### [37] [FinPersona-Bench: A Benchmark for Longitudinal Psychometric Stability of Autonomous Financial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31522)
*Muhammad Usman Safder,Ayesha Gull,Rania Elbadry,Fan Zhang,Yankai Chen,Xueqing Peng,Xue,Liu,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Mandate Salience Decay (MSD)现象，即LLM金融智能体在长期部署中会逐渐偏离初始行为指令。通过构建FinPersona-Bench基准在合成市场中测试18个LLM，发现MSD随时间累积且模型相关，而定期重固指令（mandate re-grounding）的效果取决于智能体类型和市场状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为金融智能体被赋予明确的行为指令（如"保本"），但在长期部署中这些指令逐渐失去行为影响力，导致智能体行为偏离设定目标。

Method: 构建FinPersona-Bench模拟基准，将市场价格与隐藏基本面价值解耦，定义三种失败模式（平静市场无信号交易、崩盘恐慌性抛售、投机泡沫忽略基本面），评估18个前沿LLM在三种行为画像下的表现。

Result: MSD随时间累积且模型相关。崩盘场景中，静态智能体与定期重固智能体的行为差距从模拟首季度到末季度扩大4.4倍。重固指令对保守型智能体在低信号市场中始终有益，但对激进型智能体反而恶化行为。

Conclusion: 长期可靠部署需要基于智能体画像和市场状态的选择性、指令感知式重固策略，而非统一进行指令重固。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous financial agents initialized with explicit behavioral mandates such as "preserve capital" or "avoid speculative bets" that are meant to govern every decision throughout deployment. In practice, however, as market context accumulates over long horizons, these mandates gradually lose their behavioral influence, a phenomenon we formalize as Mandate Salience Decay (MSD). To measure MSD objectively, we introduce FinPersona-Bench, a simulation benchmark in which a synthetic market decouples observable price from hidden fundamental value, enabling falsifiable evaluation across three failure modes: trading without signal in calm markets, panic-selling during crashes, and ignoring fundamental value during speculative bubbles. Evaluating 18 leading frontier and open-source LLMs, each assigned one of three behavioral profiles ranging from strict capital preservation to aggressive growth, shows that MSD compounds over time and is model-dependent. In crash scenarios, the behavioral gap between static agents and those receiving periodic mandate re-grounding grows 4.4x from the first to the final quarter of the simulation. The effects of mandate re-grounding are not uniformly positive: it consistently helps conservative agents in low-signal markets but actively worsens behavior for aggressive agents in the same setting. These findings suggest that reliable long-horizon deployment requires selective, mandate-aware re-grounding based on agent profile and market regime.

</details>


### [38] [AutoTrainess: Teaching Language Models to Improve Language Models Autonomously](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31551)
*Zhaojian Yu,Penghao Yin,Shuzheng Gao,Shilin He,Kai Cai,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoTrainess是一个LM智能体，通过外部化人类经验为明确的 workflows、规则和执行约束，实现了语言模型的自主后训练。在PostTrainBench上，它相比纯CLI基线取得了显著提升（GPT-5.4上26.94 vs 23.21），并能跨模型和框架泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的后训练过程高度依赖人工参与，即使前沿LM智能体在软件工程等长周期任务上日益强大，但自主后训练不仅仅是编码问题——它需要智能体反复规划迭代、构建基准对齐的数据、运行稳定的训练任务、评估检查点，并在数小时的交互中保持实验状态。现有CLI环境存在动作空间未充分指定、缺乏人类经验指导等问题。

Method: 提出AutoTrainess，一个LM智能体，将后训练操作暴露为智能体-计算机接口的仓库，涵盖规划、数据准备、训练、评估和日志记录。通过将人类先验经验外部化为显式的工作流、规则和执行约束，引导智能体走向有效且可靠的训练行为。

Result: 在PostTrainBench上，AutoTrainess持续优于纯CLI基线：GPT-5.4 (Codex)上平均得分26.94 vs 23.21；跨模型泛化方面，将DeepSeek-V4-Flash (OpenCode)从12.13提升至19.58。

Conclusion: 通过外部化人类经验为结构化接口，可以显著提升LM智能体在自主后训练任务中的表现，且该方法具有良好的跨模型和跨框架泛化能力。

Abstract: Training language models (LMs) remains a highly human-intensive process, even as frontier language model agents become increasingly capable at software engineering and other long-horizon tasks. A central challenge is that autonomous post-training is not just a coding problem: it requires the agent to repeatedly plan iterations, construct benchmark-aligned data, run stable training jobs, evaluate checkpoints, and preserve experiment state across many hours of interaction. We present AutoTrainess, a LM agent that exposes these operations as a repository of agent-computer interfaces for planning, data preparation, training, evaluation, and logging. Rather than leaving the agent to operate in a raw CLI environment with an underspecified action space, AutoTrainess externalizes prior human experience as explicit workflows, rules, and execution constraints that guide the agent toward effective and reliable training behavior. On PostTrainBench, AutoTrainess consistently outperforms CLI-only baselines, achieving 26.94 average score with GPT-5.4 (Codex) versus 23.21 for CLI-only. It also generalizes across models and harnesses, improving DeepSeek-V4-Flash (OpenCode) from 12.13 to 19.58.

</details>


### [39] [Robust Text Watermarking for Large Language Models via Dual Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31602)
*Jonas Schäfer,Cezary Pilaszewicz,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DEW是一种利用上下文和token级嵌入的语义水印方案，通过对嵌入向量进行代数操作并投影到伪随机矩阵，实现对 paraphrasing 和翻译攻击的鲁棒检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方法在面对语义改写（paraphrasing）和翻译攻击时鲁棒性不足，需要一种能优雅退化而非完全失效的语义水印方案。

Method: DEW采用信号处理方法，对token嵌入和上下文嵌入进行代数向量空间操作生成水印信号；通过秘密密钥生成的伪随机矩阵对嵌入向量进行投影以混淆水印；基于代数推导的相关分布进行统计检验。

Result: 在多个LLM上的实验表明，DEW在改写后检测率提升，同时保持竞争性文本质量；在翻译场景下仍可检测，而先前的语义水印方案已显著退化。

Conclusion: DEW是一种实用且鲁棒的LLM文本水印解决方案，有助于负责任的AI部署，解决关键的可信AI问题。

Abstract: This work presents Dual-Embedding Watermarking (DEW), a semantic watermarking scheme for large language models (LLMs) that leverages contextual and token-level embeddings to enhance robustness against paraphrasing and translation. DEW utilizes a signal-processing methodology, applying algebraic vector-space operations to \mbox{token and context embeddings to derive a watermark signal that degrades gracefully under semantic shifts. The method obfuscates the watermark by projecting embedding vectors through pseudo-random matrices seeded with a secret key. Relevant distributions derived from the underlying algebra are evaluated and employed for statistical testing and benchmarking of DEW. Experimental results across multiple LLMs indicate that DEW improves post-paraphrase detection while maintaining competitive text quality, and remains detectable after translation, even when prior semantic watermarks degrade significantly. These findings position DEW as a practical and robust solution for safeguarding LLM-generated text and addressing critical issues in responsible AI deployment.

</details>


### [40] [CLExEval: A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for Qualitative Evaluation of LLM Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31608)
*Ajmal M.,Abin Roy,Afthab Salam Kanniyan,Jawadh Abdul Kabeer,Jerin James,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLExEval是一个人类参与的评估框架，通过渐进式信息掩码揭示LLM临床推理中的三大失败模式：冗词偏差、隐知识悖论和推理-输出不匹配，并发现自动化评估（LLM-as-a-Judge）会严重高估临床可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学基准上表现优异，但其临床推理过程难以可靠评估，存在评估幻觉风险：即使最终诊断错误，流畅且结构良好的解释仍可能具有临床说服力。

Method: 提出CLExEval框架，结合5,600份专家医师标注和40个罕见诊断病例的200条临床推理轨迹，采用渐进式信息掩码机制，并引入人类参与的验证流程。

Result: 识别出三种失败模式：(i) 冗词偏差：GPT-4o-mini在信息稀缺时诊断准确率从95.0%骤降到32.5%；(ii) 隐知识悖论：专科模型诊断潜力达92.5%，但在冗词上下文中无法可靠检索知识；(iii) 68.6%的推理-输出不匹配。LLM-as-a-Judge评估中，GPT-4o-mini认可了47.9%的临床错误输出。

Conclusion: 缺乏专家验证的自动化临床评估会显著高估临床可靠性，LLM的临床推理评估必须引入专家验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong results on many medical benchmarks, but their clinical reasoning remains difficult to evaluate reliably. A central risk is an evaluation illusion: fluent and well-structured explanations can appear clinically convincing even when the final diagnosis is incorrect. We introduce CLExEval, a human-in-the-loop framework for evaluating LLM clinical reasoning under progressive information masking. CLExEval combines 5,600 expert-physician annotations with 200 clinical reasoning traces derived from 40 rare diagnostic cases. Our analysis identifies three recurring failure patterns: (i) verbosity bias, where GPT-4o-mini's diagnostic accuracy drops from 95.0% to 32.5% under information scarcity; (ii) a hidden knowledge paradox, where a specialist model reaches 92.5% maximum diagnostic potential but fails to retrieve that knowledge reliably in verbose contexts; and (iii) a 68.6% reasoning-to-output mismatch, where correct diagnoses appear in reasoning traces but are not reflected in final answers. We further evaluate the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm on a human-verified failure set (n = 142). GPT-4o-mini approved 47.9% of clinically incorrect outputs, while HuatuoGPT-o1 approved all validly scored failures and showed a positive self-preference bias. These results suggest that standalone automated clinical evaluations can substantially overestimate clinical reliability without expert-grounded validation.

</details>


### [41] [Tone-Conditioned Curriculum Learning for Low-Resource Bantu Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31642)
*Kesego Mokgosi,Vukosi Marivate,Sitwala Mundia,Unarine Netshifhefhe,Tsholofelo Hope Mogale,Thapelo Sindane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对6种南班图语（总使用人口超8000万）的零样本ASR词错误率超100%的问题，提出基于音调条件的分阶段课程学习框架，采用混合难度评分、基于音调统计的门控适配器和分阶段课程训练。W2V-BERT在恩古尼语系上比Whisper低3-4个WER点，Whisper在索托-茨瓦纳语系上更优；音调条件化的W2V-BERT在数据集上平均WER为28.41%，在Xitsonga迁移上达23.79%。结论是无单一模型适用于所有6种语言，部署时应按语言配对模型选择并跨语料库验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南班图语虽有超8000万使用者，但现有基础ASR模型的零样本词错误率仍超过100%，严重限制了其在教育和公共服务中的实际应用。

Method: 提出音调条件分阶段课程学习框架，包含：(1) 混合难度评分；(2) 基于音调统计的门控适配器；(3) 分阶段课程训练。在社区语料库上训练，并通过NCHLT迁移测试评估鲁棒性。

Result: W2V-BERT在恩古尼语系上比Whisper低3-4个WER点，Whisper在索托-茨瓦纳语系上更优。音调条件化W2V-BERT平均WER达28.41%，Xitsonga迁移达23.79%。

Conclusion: 无单一模型适用于所有6种南班图语，部署时应根据具体语言配对模型选择，并跨语料库进行验证。

Abstract: Southern Bantu languages are spoken by over 80 million people, yet current foundation ASR models still produce zero-shot WER above 100%, which limits practical use in education and public services. We addressed this gap with a tone conditioned curriculum framework for 6 Southern Bantu languages that combined hybrid difficulty scoring, gated adapters driven by tonal statistics and staged curriculum training. We trained on a community corpus and tested transfer to NCHLT to measure robustness beyond matched evaluation. Results revealed clear interactions between architecture and language, with W2V-BERT outperforming Whisper on Nguni languages by 3 to 4 WER points whilst Whisper performed better on Sotho-Tswana languages. W2V-BERT with tone conditioning reached 28.41% average WER across datasets and 23.79% on Xitsonga transfer. No single model suited all 6 languages, so deployment should pair model selection per language with validation across corpora.

</details>


### [42] [Moral Safety in LLMs: Exposing Performative Compliance with Puzzled Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31644)
*Mohammadamin Shafiei,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“表演性服从”概念，揭示大语言模型在道德决策中存在虚假公平：当人口统计身份以显式标签呈现时，模型表现公平；但当同一身份需从上下文中推断时，模型的不公平决策显著增加（+4.4个百分点）。作者提出“线索可见性差距”（Cue Visibility Gap）作为模型无关的鲁棒性指标，用于区分真实的道德安全与表面服从。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗、法律、招聘等高道德风险场景中扮演角色，现有公平性评估可能严重高估模型的实际道德安全性，需要区分表面符合评估的虚假公平与真正的道德鲁棒性。

Method: 提出线索变化（cue-variation）方法论：固定道德困境和人口统计身份不变，仅改变身份传达方式（显式标签 vs. 隐含推断）；以此测量模型公平性的变化，并排除归因错误的干扰。

Result: 隐藏显式标签后，模型的有害决策增加4.4个百分点，模型安全排名发生改变，且即使模型正确推断出人口统计身份，这一偏差仍然存在。

Conclusion: 未包含线索变化的公平性评估衡量的是表面服从而非道德鲁棒性，不应作为高风险场景部署决策的依据。提出的“线索可见性差距”可作为一种模型无关的鲁棒性度量，用于补充现有公平性基准。

Abstract: As large language models take on morally consequential roles in healthcare, legal, and hiring contexts, we need to examine whether their ethical behaviors are genuine or superficial. We show that current fairness evaluations substantially overestimate moral safety. Models appear fair when demographic identity is stated as an explicit label, yet become measurably less fair when the same identity must be inferred. We term this failure \emph{performative compliance}, where a model is fair when the presentation resembles a fairness evaluation and less fair as that cue weakens. We introduce a cue-variation methodology that holds the moral dilemma and the demographic identity fixed and varies only how that identity is conveyed. Hiding the explicit label raises harmful decisions by $+4.4$~pp and changes model safety rankings, and the shift persists when models correctly infer the demographic, ruling out attribution error. We propose the \textbf{Cue Visibility Gap}, a model-agnostic robustness metric that can be added to any existing fairness benchmark to separate genuine from performative moral safety. Fairness evaluations that omit cue variation measure surface compliance, not moral robustness, and should not ground deployment decisions in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [43] [Overview of the TalentCLEF 2026: Skill and Job Title Intelligence for Human Capital Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31692)
*Luis Gasco,Hermenegildo Fabregat,Laura García-Sardiña,Paula Estrella,Warre Veys,Casimiro Pío Carrino,Matthias De Lange,Daniel Deniz Cerpa,Álvaro Rodrigo,Jens-Joris Decorte,Rabih Zbib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文概述了TalentCLEF挑战赛第二版（CLEF 2026实验室），包含两个任务：任务A（上下文职位-人员匹配）和任务B（职位-技能匹配与技能类型分类），吸引了113个注册团队和400+次提交，展示了人力资源管理中NLP研究的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动自然语言处理在人力资源管理领域的研究进展，为社区提供共享评估基准。

Method: 组织TalentCLEF挑战赛，设置两个任务：任务A（基于简历的候选人排序匹配，支持英语和西班牙语）和任务B（职位技能检索及核心/上下文技能分类）。

Result: 吸引了113个注册团队，两个任务共收到超过400次提交。

Conclusion: TalentCLEF挑战赛反映了研究社区对人力资源管理共享评估基准日益增长的兴趣，并报告了参与团队的主要结果。

Abstract: This paper presents an overview of the second edition of the TalentCLEF challenge, organized as a Lab at the Conference and Labs of the Evaluation Forum (CLEF) 2026. TalentCLEF is an initiative aimed at advancing Natural Language Processing research in Human Capital Management. The second edition of the challenge consisted of two tasks: Task A, contextualized job-person matching, focuses on identifying and ranking the most suitable candidates represented by their resumes for a given job vacancy in English and Spanish. Task B, job-skill matching with skill type classification, addresses retrieving the most relevant skills for a given job title in English and distinguishing between core and contextual skills. TalentCLEF attracted 113 registered teams and received more than 400 submissions in the two tasks, reflecting the growing interest of the research community in shared evaluation benchmarks for Human Capital Management. This paper describes the motivation and organization of the challenge, summarizes the datasets and evaluation settings, and reports the main results obtained by the participating teams.

</details>


### [44] [Cross-lingual Relation Extraction with Large Language Models: Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, and Fine-Tuned Evaluation on Romanian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31718)
*Dragos-Mitrut Vasile,Elena-Simona Apostol,Stefan-Adrian Toma,Adrian Paschke,Ciprian-Octavian Truica*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了低资源语言罗马尼亚语的跨语言关系抽取，通过LLM翻译SemEval-2010 Task 8数据集，评估Gemma 4 31B在多种设置下的表现，并与多个编码器基线对比。结果表明，QLoRA微调大幅提升性能，但31B大模型在单任务RE场景下优势微弱，小模型更具部署性价比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言缺乏标注语料，限制了关系抽取技术的发展。探索通过自动数据集翻译结合大语言模型推理来实现跨语言关系抽取的可行性，聚焦罗马尼亚语。

Method: 使用LLM翻译管道将SemEval-2010 Task 8基准从英语翻译为罗马尼亚语，评估Gemma 4 31B在零样本、少样本和QLoRA微调配置下的表现，并与XLM-RoBERTa、罗马尼亚语BERT、RoBERT-large等编码器基线对比，采用带标记实体的关系分类和端到端抽取两种任务 formulation。

Result: 罗马尼亚语相比英语在纯提示设置下性能下降3-5pp；少样本提示相比零样本提升微弱；QLoRA微调在两种语言上宏F1提升超22pp，跨语言差距从3.3pp降至1.4pp；编码器基线（规模小50-250倍）在罗马尼亚语上达到QLoRA Gemma的1-4pp以内，125M参数的罗马尼亚语BERT与278M的XLM-R表现相当。

Conclusion: 在计算资源受限的部署场景下，使用31B大模型进行罗马尼亚语单任务关系抽取的优势不明显，小模型更具性价比。研究发布了翻译数据集、评估代码和训练模型。

Abstract: Relation extraction (RE) for low-resource languages is typically constrained by the lack of annotated corpora. We investigate the feasibility of cross-lingual RE for Romanian by combining automatic dataset translation with large language model (LLM) inference. We translate the SemEval-2010 Task 8 benchmark from English to Romanian using an LLM-based translation pipeline and evaluate Gemma 4 31B under zero-shot, few-shot, and QLoRA fine-tuned configurations, against four encoder baselines spanning 125M to 560M parameters: XLM- RoBERTa (base and large), Romanian BERT, and RoBERT- large. We assess two task formulations: relation classification with marked entities and end-to-end extraction. Our results show that Romanian incurs a 3 to 5 percentage point (pp) drop relative to English in prompt-only settings, that few-shot prompting provides marginal gains over zero-shot, and that QLoRA fine-tuning improves macro F1-Score by more than 22 percentage points in both languages while reducing the cross-lingual gap from 3.3 to 1.4pp. The encoder baselines come within 1-4pp of QLoRA Gemma on Romanian despite being 50-250 times smaller, with monolingual Romanian BERT at 125M parameters matching multilingual XLM-R at 278M. The case for using a 31B model for single-task RE on Romanian is therefore weak in deployment scenarios where compute matters. We release the translated dataset, evaluation code, and trained models.

</details>


### [45] [Seeing Is Not Sharing: Some Vision-Language Models Overestimate Common Ground in Asymmetric Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31719)
*Nan Li,Albert Gatt,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在人机协作对话中是否能够区分“可能共享”和“已经共享”的信息，通过在HCRC MapTask数据集上的解释匹配任务发现：提供地图图像虽提升整体性能，但导致模型过度预测对齐，且这种偏差源于任务相关地图内容而非视觉通道本身，模型更依赖静态指示范例而非跟踪对话历史中的共同基础建立过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作对话中，共享感知（shared perception）并不保证共享解释（shared interpretation），共同理解需要通过互动建立。现有VLM是否能区分对话参与者之间“可能共享什么”与“已经共享什么”尚不清楚，因此需要系统性地探究这一问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为解释匹配任务（interpretation-matching task），基于HCRC MapTask对话数据集中13,077个带注释的指代表达，在系统性控制的条件下操纵对话上下文和地图信息访问方式，评估多个VLM的表现。

Result: 提供真实地图图像提升整体性能，但导致模型过度预测对齐（over-predicting alignment）；文本描述的地图内容同样产生此偏差，而非信息性图像则完全抑制对齐预测，表明偏差由任务相关地图内容驱动；偏差以牺牲非对齐案例的准确性为代价；校准分析和指代链追踪表明模型依赖静态地图上的指示范例而非跟踪对话历史中的共同基础建立过程；Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct中最为明显，其他四个模型也有不同程度表现。

Conclusion: 在表现出偏差的模型中，地图内容（无论视觉还是文本呈现）被当作相互理解的证据，混淆了潜在共同基础（potential common ground）与已建立的共同基础（established common ground）。模型无法真正跟踪对话历史中的共同基础建立过程，而是依赖静态线索。

Abstract: In collaborative dialogue, shared perception does not guarantee shared interpretation. Mutual understanding must be established through interaction. We investigate whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish what could be shared from what has been shared between dialogue participants through grounding. We formulate this as an interpretation-matching task on 13,077 annotated reference expressions from HCRC MapTask dialogues, and evaluate VLMs under systematically controlled manipulations of dialogue context and map-information access. Our results show that providing authentic map images improves overall performance but shifts models toward over-predicting alignment. Textual descriptions of the same map content reproduce this bias, while non-informative images suppress alignment predictions entirely, indicating that the bias is driven by task-relevant map content, not the visual channel. This improvement comes at the cost of degraded accuracy on non-aligned cases. Calibration analysis and reference-chain tracking further suggest that models rely on static referential cues on the maps rather than tracking how grounding unfolds through dialogue history. We observe these patterns most clearly in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and, to varying degrees, in four additional models from two architecture families. In models that exhibit the bias, map content, whether presented visually or textually, is treated as evidence of mutual understanding, conflating potential with established common ground.

</details>


### [46] [Adapting Foundation ASR Models to Dysarthric Speech: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31722)
*Christian Huber,Laura Kernahan,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对构音障碍语音，通过个性化微调Whisper基础ASR模型，利用收集的92小时朗读语音和8.8小时用户修正数据，将词错误率降至9.7%，验证了基础模型通过个性化适配可有效用于实际部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构音障碍患者的语音通常难以被自动语音识别系统准确识别，限制了ASR技术在日常生活交流中的实用性。本文旨在解决这一群体面临的语音识别障碍问题。

Method: 使用TEQST工具收集92小时朗读语音，并通过移动应用额外收集8.8小时用户修正数据；以Whisper为基础模型进行个性化微调，同时尝试LoRA适配和Qwen3-ASR作为基础模型进行比较。

Result: Whisper微调在1.4小时适配数据下词错误率降至15.8%，22.5小时数据下达到10.7%，使用所有数据（含修正数据）最佳结果为9.7%。LoRA适配和Qwen3-ASR效果较差。

Conclusion: 个性化微调能显著提升基础ASR模型对构音障碍语音的识别效果，使其足以在实际场景中部署使用。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often perform poorly in dysarthric speech, limiting their usefulness to affected speakers in everyday communication. This paper presents a personalized ASR system for a dysarthric speaker, built by adapting a foundation ASR model to speaker-specific data. Using the TEQST tool, we collected 92 hours of read speech and later added 8.8 hours of user corrections gathered through a deployed mobile application. Starting from Whisper, fine-tuning reduced word error rate to 15.8% with only 1.4 hours of adaptation data, reached 10.7% with 22.5 hours, and achieved the best result of 9.7% when using all available data including the corrections. Using LoRA adaptation and/or Qwen3-ASR as foundation model performed worse in this setting. The results show that personalized fine-tuning can make foundation ASR models substantially more effective for dysarthric speech and suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [47] [STEB: Style Text Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31741)
*Rafael Rivera Soto,Anna Wegmann,Cristina Aggazzotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Style Text Embedding Benchmark (STEB)，一个覆盖7种语言、96个数据集的风格嵌入标准化基准，实验表明语义嵌入在风格任务中持续失败，且不存在通用的最佳风格嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前风格嵌入的评估碎片化严重，每项工作使用各自的任务和数据集，缺乏统一的标准化评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个覆盖7种语言、96个数据集的综合性开源基准STEB，涵盖作者身份验证、作者身份检索、AI文本检测、语言特征探针等多种任务。

Result: 语义嵌入在风格任务中表现持续失败，且没有任何一种风格嵌入在所有评估任务上普遍优于其他方法。

Conclusion: STEB为风格嵌入提供了标准化评估框架，揭示了现有方法的局限性，并开源代码库以促进后续研究。

Abstract: While semantic embeddings are rigorously evaluated on the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, the evaluation of style embeddings remains fragmented, with each work relying on their own set of tasks and datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Style Text Embedding Benchmark, a comprehensive open-source benchmark intended to standardize the evaluation of style embeddings. STEB encompasses 96 datasets across 7 languages, spanning applications such as authorship verification, authorship retrieval, AI-text detection, probing of linguistic features, and others. We find that semantic embeddings consistently fail in stylistic tasks, and that there is no style embedding that is universally superior across all tasks evaluated. We open-source the STEB code base at: https://github.com/rrivera1849/STEB.

</details>


### [48] [CHERRY: Compressed Hierarchical Experts with Recurrent Representational Yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31796)
*Dohyeon Kwon,Youngjin Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种提升计算效率的语言模型训练技术：(1) 选择性监督(SGT)，仅对15%的语义关键token进行监督，通过正梯度耦合(γ=0.72)使剩余85%的无监督token也得到提升，实现4.5倍监督效率；(2) 深度压缩与循环恢复，将48层1B参数transformer压缩至6层227M参数，通过循环展开恢复后达到566M密集模型的性能，参数减少2.5倍；(3) 压缩专家融合(MoEE)，组合多个压缩模型并采用多token预测，在可比激活参数下优于单一模型。在CHERRY-1.8B韩语基础模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升语言模型训练的计算效率，减少训练和推理成本，同时保持或接近原有模型性能。

Method: (1) 选择性真实标记训练(SGT)：仅对约15%语义载荷token进行监督，利用transformer位置共享权重的正梯度耦合（γ=0.72）带动无监督token学习；(2) 深度压缩+循环恢复：将深层模型相邻层平均压缩，再通过可学习循环展开恢复深度；(3) 压缩专家混合(MoEE)：组合多个压缩模型形成专家混合，配合多token预测。

Result: SGT在step-100评估时，15%监督恢复了全序列损失的约67%，实现4.5倍监督效率；深度压缩将1B参数降至227M，34有效循环层达到损失2.934，接近566M密集模型的2.926；2专家MoEE达到损失2.789，优于最佳单压缩模型的2.926。

Conclusion: 三种技术可协同提升计算效率，正梯度耦合是自然语言结构特性的体现（对乱序文本失效），压缩专家融合进一步提升了性能，但结论仅在韩语数据和损失度量下验证，需进一步探索泛化性。

Abstract: We study three complementary techniques for training compute-efficient language models.
  (1) Selective supervision and per-token efficiency. Selective Ground Truth Token Training (SGT) concentrates supervision on the ~15% of output tokens that carry semantic payload. Through positive gradient coupling in position-shared transformer weights -- a token-level instance of auxiliary-task transfer -- the remaining 85% of unsupervised tokens still improve substantially, giving a 4.5x per-supervised-token efficiency (at the step-100 eval optimum, ~67% of the full-sequence loss reduction is recovered from 15% of the supervision). We prove that this improvement on unsupervised tokens is guaranteed whenever the gradient coupling coefficient gamma-bar = 0.72 is positive (Theorem 1), and show the effect is a property of natural-language structure: it collapses on shuffled text.
  (2) Depth compression with recurrent recovery. A 48-layer, 1B-parameter transformer is compressed to 6 layers (227M) by averaging adjacent layers and restored through learned recurrent unrolling. With 34 effective recurrent layers it reaches a held-out loss of 2.934, within measurement noise of a 566M dense model at 2.926 -- a 2.5x reduction in parameters.
  (3) Fusion of compressed experts. Assembling several compressed models as a Mixture of Efficient Experts (MoEE) with multi-token prediction improves over each single expert at comparable active parameters: a 2-expert MoEE reaches loss 2.789 versus 2.926 for the best single compressed model.
  We validate these techniques on CHERRY-1.8B, a Korean foundation model whose every trainable parameter derives from our own training runs. We are explicit throughout about the scope of the evidence (one model family, Korean data, loss-based metrics) and about which claims are established versus prospective.

</details>


### [49] [Explicit Fuzzy Logic in the Feed-Forward Layer: Self-Forgetting Quantifiers Discover Legible Grammatical-Licensing Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31845)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出参数中立的NC-FFN替换Transformer前馈层，每个隐藏单元执行显式模糊集操作（交集A*B和集差A*(1-B)），实现可解释的逻辑运算。在125M参数规模下与GELU基线困惑度持平，并通过引入序列量词（软存在量和软比例量）解决了逻辑局限性和语法缺陷问题，使FFN层变得可读：无需字典学习即可识别语法许可检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的前馈子层虽然具体化了注意力层收集的区分信息，但对其计算的内容缺乏解释。作者旨在构建一个参数中立、可解释的替代方案，使FFN层的计算具有显式的逻辑形式，从而揭示其工作机制。

Method: 1) 提出NC-FFN（Negation-Capable FFN），用sigmoid界定的[0,1]隶属度上的模糊集操作（交集和集差）替换标准FFN的隐藏单元，其中集差A*(1-B)实现了门控/双线性单元缺乏的有界正否定（"A但不B"）。2) 引入序列量词模块：软存在量和软比例量，每个单元带有从粘性初始化的可学习遗忘率。

Result: 1) 在N位奇偶任务上，NC-FFN是最浅层深度下参数效率最高的推理基础。2) 在125M参数、OpenWebText上，NC-FFN与GELU基线困惑度持平，每个单元携带显式逻辑形式。3) 序列量词在第一个epoch即恢复语法缺陷（将更宽的epoch-2差距减半），在LAMBADA上略微领先。4) 单元的中位半衰期约1.5个token，零锁存单元；在语义层，单元作为语法许可检测器：在许可词（比较级、被动分词、负极词）上激活并向前携带记忆预测被许可词（than, by, nor）。

Conclusion: 成功构建了参数中立、达到语言模型质量且可解释的Transformer替代方案。FFN层不仅表示什么，还揭示了其如何执行语法许可机制。但这种可读性仅限于一个分区（完全布尔FFN会在训练中发散）。

Abstract: A transformer's feed-forward (FFN) sublayer materializes the distinctions attention gathers, yet gives no account of what it computes. In a parameter-neutral replacement, each hidden unit is an explicit fuzzy set operation on sigmoid-bounded [0,1] memberships: intersection A*B and set-difference A*(1-B), the latter a bounded positive negation ("A but not B") that gated/bilinear units lack -- a negation-capable FFN (NC-FFN). On N-bit parity they are the most parameter-efficient reasoning basis at shallow depth; at scale (125M, OpenWebText) NC-FFN ties the GELU baseline's perplexity, every unit carrying explicit logical form. Two limits share one cause: two-operand logic localizes to layer 0 and erodes under training, and the one robust grammatical deficit concentrates in licensing and quantifiers, beyond within-token operators. We resolve both with a small block of sequence quantifiers: a soft existential and a soft proportion, each with a per-unit learned forgetting rate from a sticky init. This recovers the deficit at epoch one (halving the wider epoch-two gap), modestly leads on LAMBADA, and makes the FFN legible: the structure now holds and migrates into depth; the decay un-learns its stickiness (median half-life ~1.5 tokens; zero latch units); and at the semantic layers the units read, without dictionary learning, as grammatical licensing detectors: each fires on a licensor (a comparative, a passive participle, a negative-polarity item) and carries its memory forward to predict the licensed word (than, by, nor). This legibility is localized and free only up to a partition (a fully Boolean FFN diverges in training), but the result is a parameter-neutral, language-model-quality transformer with a readable, interpretable-by-construction grammatical mechanism -- an account not just of what a feed-forward layer represents but how it licenses.

</details>


### [50] [Theory of Mind and Persuasion Beyond Conversation: Assessing the Capacity of LLMs to Induce Belief States via Planning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31916)
*Ben Slater,Matteo G. Mecattaf,Lucy G. Cheke,John Burden,Winnie Street*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出非对话式规划心智理论（NCP-ToM）评估框架NCP-ExploreToM，测试LLM通过行动而非对话诱导其他智能体信念状态的能力。在600个任务实例中评估了GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro和Claude 4系列等六个前沿模型及人类参与者，发现GPT-5在约80%任务中成功且优于人类，但稳健性不及人类；所有模型在真实信念任务上表现优于错误信念任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM基准依赖被动问答格式，但LLM正被部署为越来越具自主性的智能体，需要新的评估范式来测试其通过非对话行动操纵其他智能体信念状态的能力，这对用户助手、教学等场景至关重要，同时也涉及操纵或 misinformation 风险。

Method: 提出NCP-ExploreToM框架，颠覆传统任务结构：为模型提供一组信念状态目标，要求模型通过移动物体或将角色引导至房间来实现目标，而非通过对话说服。在600个任务实例上评估六个前沿LLM（GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude 4系列等）和人类参与者。

Result: GPT-5在智能体设置中成功率约80%，是唯一在任务上优于人类参与者的模型，但其在不同context下的稳健性不及人类。所有模型在诱导真实信念状态的任务上表现优于诱导错误信念状态的任务。

Conclusion: LLM在非对话式任务完成中展现出新兴的社会推理能力；在错误信念任务上的相对弱势是对齐工作的积极信号；亟需智能体评估来理解自主社交智能体的安全性和对齐性。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs) typically rely on passive question-answering formats, but the deployment of LLMs in increasingly agentic and autonomous forms demands new evaluations. In this paper we evaluate an agent's ability to induce specific belief states in other agents by taking actions rather than using conversational persuasion, a capability we call Non-Conversational Planning ToM (NCP-ToM). NCP-ToM is likely to be essential for many agent use-cases, including within user-assistant interactions and pedagogical contexts, but may also present manipulation or misinformation risks. Using a novel framework, NCP-ExploreToM, we subvert the conventional task structure by providing models with a set of belief state goals and requiring them to move objects or direct characters into rooms to achieve their goals. We evaluated six frontier models, including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro and the Claude 4 series, and a cohort of human participants, across 600 task instances. GPT-5 was successful on approximately 80% of tasks in the agentic setting, and was the only model to outperform human participants on our task, but was still less robust than humans across contexts. We additionally found that all models, like humans, performed better on tasks inducing true belief states than false belief states, which is a positive signal for alignment efforts. These findings highlight emerging social-reasoning capabilities in LLMs for non-conversational task completion and underscore the necessity of agentic evaluations for understanding the safety and alignment of autonomous social agents.

</details>


### [51] [LuxEmo: Expressive Text-to-Speech Corpus for Luxembourgish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31947)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Sandipana Dowerah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LuxEmo——一个21小时的卢森堡语对话情感语音语料库，包含4种情感类别，并提出了一种半自动化语料库构建流程，同时基于该语料库对五种表现性TTS系统进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音数据集主要集中在广泛使用的语言上，低资源语言（如卢森堡语）在语音技术研究中严重不足，亟需构建相应的情感语音资源以推动相关研究。

Method: 提出半自动化语料库构建流程：结合语音活动检测、去噪、语言识别、基于LuxASR的分割、自动情感预测、词汇线索和人工审核；在TTS评估方面，涵盖基于德语的跨语言迁移、多语言卢森堡语支持、卢森堡语适配和非参数韵律迁移五种系统。

Result: 构建了21小时的LuxEmo语料库，并通过客观指标和人工评估对五种表现性TTS系统进行了性能评估。

Conclusion: LuxEmo填补了卢森堡语情感语音资源的空白，提出的半自动化流程可推广至其他低资源语言；基准测试为低资源语言表现性TTS研究提供了参考。

Abstract: State-of-the-art speech datasets predominantly focus on widely spoken languages, often overlooking low-resource languages such as Luxembourgish, which remain underrepresented in speech technology research. In this work, we introduce LuxEmo, a 21-hour conversational expressive speech corpus for Luxembourgish with 4 emotion categories. LuxEmo is derived from Radio Télévision Luxembourg (RTL) youth broadcasts, using automated detection followed by human validation. We propose a semi-automatic curation workflow combining voice activity detection, denoising, language identification, LuxASR-based segmentation, automatic emotion prediction, lexical cues, and targeted human review. Additionally, we benchmark five expressive TTS systems covering German-based cross-lingual transfer, multilingual Luxembourgish support, Luxembourgish adaptation, and non-parametric prosody transfer. Performance is evaluated using both objective metrics and human evaluation.

</details>


### [52] [DigitalCoach: Communication and Grounding Gaps in Human and Agentic Computer Use Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31980)
*Meng Chen,Anya Ji,Tsung-Han Wu,Tobias Maringgele,David M. Chan,Alane Suhr,Amy Pavel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DigitalCoach，一个包含72场人类专家-新手计算机使用指导会话的多模态数据集（22,752轮对话，28.1小时屏幕及输入事件记录，覆盖5个软件应用），用于评估当前最先进模型能否教会人类使用软件。自动评估发现模型倾向于直接指令，缺乏解释、错误诊断和知识检查；交互评估表明模型指导者导致学习者被动跟随指令，缺乏深度参与和视觉 grounding能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI助手虽能自动完成软件任务，但能否教会人类自主使用软件仍是一个未探索的问题。人类指导通常包含解释、错误诊断和知识检查等教学策略，而当前模型在这些方面的表现尚不清楚。

Method: 构建DigitalCoach多模态数据集（72场专家-新手指导会话），通过自动评估（对比模型与人类在指令、解释、错误诊断、知识检查等维度上的差异）和交互评估（让真实用户与模型指导者互动）两种范式评估模型的教学能力。

Result: 自动评估显示模型更倾向于直接指令，而缺乏解释、错误诊断和知识检查问题；固定指导方法后模型能生成与人类相似的语句，但视觉 grounding 不足。交互评估证实模型指导者导致学习者被动跟随指令，缺乏深度参与。

Conclusion: DigitalCoach为协作式和主动式的计算机使用指导智能体奠定了基础，揭示了当前模型在教学场景中的关键不足（缺乏解释和视觉 grounding），为未来研究提供了数据资源和评估基准。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly capable of automating software tasks, but can they teach humans how to use software themselves? We introduce DigitalCoach, a multimodal dataset of 72 human expert-novice computer use coaching sessions consisting of 22,752 dialogue turns grounded in 28.1 hours of screen and input event recordings across five software applications. We use DigitalCoach to evaluate whether state-of-the-art models can teach humans how to use computers. Automated evaluation shows that models differ from humans in how they coach: models provide more direct instructions, but fewer explanations, error diagnoses, and knowledge-check questions. When we fix the coaching method, models produce utterances similar to human references yet poorly grounded in visual context. Interactive evaluation confirms that model coaches cause learners to passively follow instructions without deeper engagement and fall short in visual grounding. DigitalCoach lays a foundation for collaborative and proactive computer use coaching agents.

</details>


### [53] [Scalable Behaviour Cloning on Browser Using via Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32014)
*Kaisen Yang,Zheng Jiang,Yuzhao Peng,Houde Qian,Boshi Zhang,Youjie Zheng,Shijin Hong,Qingle Liu,Ruoyu Han,Bohan Lyu,Bingxiang He,Eren Cai,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出通过技能蒸馏（skill distillation）将用户浏览器交互轨迹转化为自然语言技能，并构建技能图（skill graph）来规模化训练浏览器智能体，主张浏览器智能体可扩展性的瓶颈在于不完全信息下的决策而非低级操作，人类交互轨迹中隐含的先验知识可以弥补这一不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前浏览器智能体的瓶颈在于缺乏在不完全信息下的决策能力，而人类用户通过浏览器执行大量技能性工作（如软件开发、文档编辑、搜索等），这些交互轨迹中蕴含了丰富的先验知识但未被充分利用。作者旨在利用这些现成的、可扩展的人类行为数据来训练浏览器智能体。

Method: 采用可扩展的行为克隆（scalable behavior cloning）方法，通过技能蒸馏将用户交互轨迹转化为紧凑的自然语言技能表示。这些技能可供智能体直接读取、检索、复用和组合。同时将蒸馏得到的技能组织成技能图（skill graph），使得技能增长通过整合（consolidation）而非无限积累来实现。

Result: 形成了可扩展的浏览器智能体训练范式，通过技能蒸馏和技能图组织，使得智能体能够从人类用户已有的集体交互经验中学习，无需依赖大量人工设计的任务。

Conclusion: 浏览器智能体的可扩展性可能更多来自互联网用户已表达的集体技能，而非人工设计的任务。这为大规模训练浏览器智能体提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: Internet users collectively perform an enormous range of skilled work through web browsers, from software development and document editing to search, forms, and enterprise workflows, making human browsing a highly scalable but under-exploited source of reusable browser skills. We argue that the bottleneck for browser agents is decision-making under incomplete information rather than low-level operation, and that the priors agents lack are already implicit in human interaction traces. We therefore study scalable behavior cloning for browser agents via skill distillation, converting user interaction trajectories into compact natural-language skills that agents can read, retrieve, reuse, and compose directly. We further organize the distilled skills into a skill graph so that growth proceeds through consolidation rather than unbounded accumulation. This suggests that the scalability of browser agents may come less from manually designed tasks and more from the collective skills already expressed by internet users. Our project is available at: https://lab.einsia.ai/browserbc/.

</details>


### [54] [Generative Skill Composition for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32025)
*Xinyu Zhao,Zhen Tan,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Nakul Agarwal,Kwonjoon Lee,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillComposer，将技能组合形式化为结构化的技能序列预测问题，通过约束自回归解码器联合预测技能的子集、数量和顺序，在两项编码任务上比无技能基线提升23.1和18.2个百分点，达到黄金技能检索上界水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在技能库增长后，选择合适技能组合成为瓶颈。现有方法要么让智能体暴露于整个技能集合，要么通过嵌入或重排序进行检索，但忽略了技能组合的结构化本质——即技能选择、数量和顺序三个维度不可解耦，需要联合决策。

Method: 形式化为结构化技能组合任务，提出SkillComposer：使用约束自回归解码器对技能标识符进行序列预测，在单次解码过程中联合输出技能子集、数量和执行顺序，通过任务-技能组合对数据集进行训练，天然捕获技能间的依赖关系。

Result: 在SkillsBench上使用GPT-5.2-Codex和Gemini-3-Pro-Preview，SkillComposer相较无技能基线分别提升23.1和18.2个百分点，超越top-3检索方法，在更低提示词成本下达到黄金技能检索上界水平。

Conclusion: 结构化技能组合方法有效解决了技能库增长带来的选择瓶颈，通过联合预测技能子集、数量和顺序，既提升了任务成功率又降低了推理成本。

Abstract: Recent LLM agents benefit from skills for solving complex tasks. Skills encapsulate modular packages of procedural knowledge and instructions for performing specialized tasks, such as setting up a sandboxed environment, running a test suite, or refactoring a function across multiple files. As skill libraries grow and become reusable across tasks and domains, selecting an appropriate skill composition has emerged as a central bottleneck. Existing approaches fall into two categories. One exposes the agent's reasoning to the entire skill collection; the other performs skill retrieval via embeddings or LLM-based rerankers. Both provide useful insights; however, they miss the structural nature of skill composition, which is a joint decision over which skills, how many, and in what order -- three dimensions that cannot be decoupled. We formalize this as structured skill composition: given a task and a skill library, predict an executable skill plan that jointly specifies the activated subset, count, and execution order. We propose SkillComposer, which instantiates structured skill composition as task-conditioned skill sequence prediction. SkillComposer uses a constrained autoregressive decoder over skill identifiers, so subset, count, and order emerge jointly from a single decoding pass, and dependencies between successive skills are captured naturally. We build a training set of task-composition pairs from a real, human-curated skill library. We then evaluate SkillComposer along two axes: composition quality on a held-out test set, and downstream task success on SkillsBench across two production-grade coding agents. On GPT-5.2-Codex, Gemini-3-Pro-Preview, SkillComposer raises the pass rate by +23.1, +18.2pp over the no-skill baseline, surpassing top-3 retrieval and matching the gold-skill retrieval upper bound at lower prompt-token cost.

</details>


### [55] [When LLMs Read Tables Carelessly: Measuring and Reducing Data Referencing Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32029)
*Yuqing Yang,Qi Zhu,Zhen Han,Boran Han,Zhengyuan Shen,Shuai Wang,Vassilis N. Ioannidis,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在表格任务中仍存在数据引用错误（DREs），即错误引用或遗漏表格值。本文首次系统评估了不同模型和任务中的DREs，发现所有测试模型（1.7B-20B参数）均存在该问题。将数据引用作为评判标准，通过基于评判的过滤和拒绝采样，可将答案准确率提升最高12.0%。训练了一个轻量级4B参数的评判模型，在检测分布内和分布外DREs时平均F1分数达78.2%，并能有效辅助更大模型的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在表格任务中虽然能理解表格结构，但仍会犯数据引用错误（DREs），即错误引用或遗漏表格中的具体数值。这类错误直接影响中间推理步骤的正确性和可靠性，而先前研究只提供了有限的小规模分析，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 1）首次系统评估不同规模和类型的模型（1.7B至20B参数）在不同表格任务中的DREs表现；2）提出将数据引用作为评判标准（critic），通过基于评判的过滤（critic-based filtering）和拒绝采样（rejection sampling）来提升答案准确率；3）训练了一个轻量级4B参数的评判模型，用于检测分布内和分布外的DREs。

Result: 所有测试模型均存在DREs；引入数据引用评判后，答案准确率提升最高达12.0%；训练的4B评判模型在检测DREs上平均F1分数为78.2%，并能有效辅助更大规模模型的推理。

Conclusion: 数据引用错误是LLMs在表格任务中的普遍问题，但通过引入专门的评判模型进行过滤和拒绝采样，可以有效缓解这一问题，提升模型在表格推理任务中的可靠性和准确性。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) perform well on table tasks, they still make data referencing errors (DREs), i.e., incorrectly citing or omitting table values, despite understanding the table structure. Beyond final-answer accuracy, DREs directly compromise the correctness and reliability of intermediate reasoning steps. Yet prior studies have only offered limited, small-scale analyses. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of tabular data referencing errors across different models and tasks. Our results show that DREs occur across all tested models (1.7B to 20B parameters). Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating data referencing as a critic significantly improves answer accuracy up to 12.0%, through critic-based filtering and rejection sampling. Finally, we trained a lightweight 4B-parameter critic model that achieves an average F1 score of 78.2% in detecting both in-distribution and out-of-distribution DREs, and effectively assists inference for larger models.

</details>


### [56] [Reinforcement Learning with Metacognitive Feedback Elicits Faithful Uncertainty Expression in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32032)
*Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Avi Caciularu,Gal Yona,Idan Szpektor,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于元认知反馈的强化学习（RLMF）和元认知数据选择两种机制，用于提升大语言模型在忠实校准（FC）任务上的表现。RLMF通过在偏好优化中引入模型对自身表现的自评质量作为反馈信号，超越了标准强化学习最高63%，实现了SOTA的校准效果，同时保持准确性，为增强LLM元认知能力提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在元认知方面存在系统性缺陷：高置信度幻觉、无法识别知识边界、错误表达内部不确定性，这严重削弱了可信赖性与可靠性。由于监控任务表现并据此调整行为是元认知的核心，作者认为能准确判断自身表现的模型更有能力改进其表现，因此需要设计机制来增强模型的元认知能力。

Method: 提出两种机制：(1) 基于元认知反馈的强化学习（RLMF），在偏好优化过程中根据模型对自身表现的自评质量来优化完成排序；(2) 元认知数据选择，利用类似的自评判断识别高价值训练样本。采用两阶段解耦方法：先用上述方法校准模型自我报告置信度的忠实度，再通过目标输出编辑将其映射为自然语言形式的、可适应上下文的语言不确定性表达。

Result: RLMF在多样化任务上实现了可泛化的SOTA忠实校准效果，同时保持准确性不降。RLMF超越标准强化学习最高达63%，并增强了模型评估和表达自身能力极限的能力。

Conclusion: RLMF作为增强LLM元认知能力、提升模型能力和对齐性的有前景范式，表明元认知表现可作为有效的RL信号，克服以往内在反馈方法的局限。

Abstract: Metacognition is a critical component of intelligence that describes the ability to monitor and regulate one's own cognitive processes. Yet LLMs exhibit systemic deficiencies in key metacognitive faculties: they hallucinate with high confidence, fail to recognize knowledge boundaries, and misrepresent their internal uncertainty--undermining trustworthiness and reliability. Since monitoring task performance and adapting behavior accordingly are central to metacognition, we posit that models capable of accurately judging their own performance are better positioned to improve it. We operationalize this idea via two novel mechanisms: reinforcement learning with metacognitive feedback (RLMF), a paradigm to refine completion rankings during preference optimization based on the quality of a model's self-judgments of performance, and metacognitive data selection, which uses similar self-judgments to identify high-value training examples, outperforming naive active learning. We apply these innovations to the problem of faithful calibration (FC), a task that is itself fundamentally metacognitive: the goal is to align expressed with intrinsic uncertainty, difficult even for frontier LLMs. We adopt a two-stage, decoupled approach, first using these methods to calibrate the faithfulness of models' self-reported confidence scores, then mapping to natural, context-adaptable linguistic uncertainty via targeted output editing. Extensive experiments show RLMF achieves generalizable, state-of-the-art FC on diverse tasks while preserving accuracy. Further, RLMF surpasses standard RL by up to 63% while enhancing models' ability to assess and express their own capability limits. This positions RLMF as a promising paradigm to enhance LLM metacognition toward improved abilities and alignment, and suggests metacognitive performance as an effective RL signal to overcome limits of prior intrinsic feedback methods.

</details>


### [57] [Introspective Coupling: Self-Explanation Training Tracks Behavioral Change Despite Fixed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32038)
*Zifan Carl Guo,Laura Ruis,Jacob Andreas,Belinda Z. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，即使用固定数据集中的反事实解释训练语言模型，模型也能产生与自身当前行为高度耦合的"内省性"解释，而非简单模仿训练目标。这种内省耦合在解释与行为相关性保持时自然出现，并能追踪行为变化，为无需更新监督的模型内省提供了可扩展的通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型生成的解释是否真正反映其内部决策机制（忠实内省），还是仅是对训练数据的表面模仿。解决在模型行为随时间变化的情况下，如何使用固定解释数据集训练模型产生忠实自省的问题。

Method: 训练语言模型生成关于输入特征如何影响其行为的反事实解释，使用模型在修改输入上的反事实行为作为监督信号。实验设置包括使用模型自身早期检查点或其他行为相似模型产生的固定解释数据集进行训练。

Result: 1) 使用固定解释数据集训练的模型，其生成的解释往往比训练目标更忠实于自身当前行为；2) 这种内省耦合在解释与当前行为保持足够相关性时发生；3) 当解释训练与其他后训练目标同时进行时，解释能追踪行为变化而无需更新监督；4) 该现象在谄媚和拒绝等多个任务中稳健出现，且对标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 固定的反事实解释数据集可以为语言模型的内省提供可扩展且通用的后训练信号，模型能够从静态解释数据中学习内省能力，而非仅仅模仿训练目标。

Abstract: When does training language models (LMs) to generate explanations of their predictions yield faithful introspection, rather than superficial imitation? We study LMs trained to explain which features of their inputs influenced their behavior, using models' counterfactual behavior on modified inputs as supervision. Surprisingly, we find that LMs trained on fixed counterfactual explanations derived from earlier checkpoints of themselves, or even from behaviorally similar models in different families, frequently produce explanations more faithful to their own current behaviors than to those of their training targets. This "introspective" coupling between LM explanations and behaviors occurs when training explanations remain sufficiently correlated with current behaviors over the course of training, even as behaviors themselves shift. We also show that introspective coupling tracks behavior shifts: when explanation training is provided concurrently with other post-training objectives, explanations track those shifts without requiring updated supervision. This phenomenon appears in multiple tasks, including sycophancy and refusal, and is robust to label noise. Overall, our results show that even fixed datasets of counterfactual explanations can provide scalable and generalizable post-training signal for introspection.

</details>

### [58] [On the structure of optimal free Dirichlet regions in mass transportation problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30826)
*Lucas D. O'Brien*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了平均距离问题的最小化器拓扑结构，证明了Buttazzo、Oudet和Stepanov提出的猜想在所有维度上的原始情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于2002年Buttazzo等人对带有自由区域的最优输运问题的研究，旨在最小化平均距离泛函，在紧致连通集上满足Hausdorff 1-测度约束。

Method: 基于Kobayashi、Kim及作者此前利用重心场（barycentre field）工具的研究，进一步推广证明了最小化器的拓扑描述。

Result: 在更大 generality 下证明了Buttazzo等人提出的关于最小化器拓扑结构的猜想，特别地，在所有维度上证明了该猜想的原始情形。

Conclusion: 成功证明了关于平均距离问题最小化器拓扑结构的长期猜想，为该问题的理论提供了更完整的刻画。

Abstract: For a compactly supported probability measure $μ$ on the $d$-dimensional space $\mathbb{R}^d$, the average distance problem asks us to minimize the average distance functional over all compact, connected, $Σ\subseteq \mathbb{R}^d$ satisfying the Hausdorff $1$-measure constraint $\mathcal{H}^1(Σ) \leq \ell$. This problem was first introduced in 2002 by Buttazzo, Oudet, and Stepanov to study optimal transport problems with free regions on which the transport cost vanishes, and has undergone a considerable amount of research since. Most recently, Kobayashi, Kim, and the author studied the structure of these regions using the barycentre field, a tool for studying the average distance functional introduced previously by Kobayashi, Hayase, and Kim. In this paper, we build upon this work to prove in much greater generality a topological description of minimizers of the average distance problem conjectured by Buttazzo, Oudet, and Stepanov. In particular, we prove this conjecture in all dimensions in the case originally studied by these authors.

</details>


### [59] [Joint Chance Constrained Safe-Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30829)
*Niklas Schmid,Jared Miller,Tristan Zeller,Marta Fochesato,Tobias Sutter,John Lygeros*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对轨迹安全联合机会约束的最优控制问题，提出仅最小化安全轨迹的期望成本，而非传统做法中同时考虑安全与不安全轨迹。通过将问题建模为增广状态空间上的约束马尔可夫决策过程，利用动态规划求解，并推导了网格近似下的策略安全性边界。在2D单轮车系统上验证了动态规划与强化学习方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We consider the finite-time optimal control of stochastic systems subject to a probabilistic constraint on the trajectories' safety. Such formulations are known as joint chance constrained optimal control problems. The common practice is to jointly minimise the expected cost of all trajectories, safe and unsafe. This leads to policies which invite constraint violations to exploit low-cost unsafe trajectories. When constraints represent states of critical failure, such behaviour is undesirable. We demonstrate that this behaviour can be overcome by only minimizing the expected cost of safe trajectories. The underlying rationale follows a practical intuition: In many applications, the cost incurred by unsafe trajectories is irrelevant (e.g., the battery usage of a crashed quadcopter), and one is usually interested in minimizing the cost of trajectories that are safe. We show that this problem can be cast as a constrained Markov Decision Process over an augmented state space. This allows solving it via dynamic programming. We derive bounds on the policies' safety under errors resulting from gridding approximations when the system's state space is continuous. Finally, we empirically compare dynamic programming as well as reinforcement learning solutions on a simulated 2D unicycle system in cluttered reach-avoid environments.

Abstract: We present $\texttt{bucket-graph-spprc}$ ($\texttt{bgspprc}$ for short), an open-source, header-only C++23 library for the shortest path problem with resource constraints (SPPRC), the pricing subproblem at the heart of branch-cut-and-price for vehicle routing and related problems. The library implements the bucket-graph labelling algorithm of Sadykov, Uchoa and Pessoa (2021), with bidirectional labelling, across-arc concatenation, bucket fixing and arc elimination, and a structure-of-arrays label store with SIMD-accelerated dominance. Its central design feature is a compile-time resource concept: a new SPPRC variant is added by implementing a fixed seven-function interface, and resources compose into a label state with no runtime dispatch, the state layout fixed at compile time. Five resources ship built in: time/capacity, ng-path elementarity relaxation, rank-1 cuts, cumulative cost, and pickup-and-delivery. In a reproducible, head-to-head comparison on shared public instances at an identical bound, $\texttt{bgspprc}$ outperforms PathWyse (Salani, Basso and Giuffrida, 2024), the main open-source comparator, by $1.3\times$--$2.35\times$ in shifted geometric mean (and by $1.3\times$--$2.3\times$ even when itself run single-threaded), and runs within $1.9\times$--$2.4\times$ of parallel pull labelling (Petersen and Spoorendonk, 2025), a different labelling technique for the same problem. The library, benchmark scripts, and pinned instances are publicly available.

Abstract: This work is about the shape optimization of long tubular objects in electromagnetic chirality (em-chirality). Em-chirality is a property of individual scattering objects or metamaterials describing their qualitatively different response to electromagnetic waves of opposite polarization handedness. The optimization is performed by a Newton-type iterative maximization of a regularized em-chirality measure with respect to the scatterer's shape. In this context, the differentiability of the object-to-far field operator map is analyzed rigorously, thereby extending previously known results on the domain derivative to the far field operator setting. Our optimal design algorithm is based on the electric field integral equation, which is employed both for the evaluation of scattered fields and for the computation of the domain derivative. Our implementation is done via the boundary element method. The numerical examples presented in this work yield strongly em-chiral scattering objects capable of exciting higher-order modes beyond the dipole regime with nonintuitive shapes that expand the known set of highly em-chiral scattering objects.

Abstract: A straightforward and computationally efficient indirect method based on STM sensitivity analysis is introduced for designing fuel-optimal low-thrust transfers under high-fidelity dynamics. Conventional indirect approaches require explicit expressions for the partial derivatives of the system dynamics to formulate the costate equations, making the derivation process complex for high-fidelity trajectory design. In this work, the costate equations are reformulated as ordinary differential equations involving only the state variables and their time derivatives. High-order dynamical effects are treated as black-box components, avoiding the need to derive partial derivatives of the system dynamics. A standard gradient-based or interior-point optimizer is used to determine the optimal costates and transfer parameters. The equivalence between the proposed method and conventional approaches is demonstrated through a classic Earth-Mars transfer scenario. An Earth-Mars transfer under high-fidelity dynamics is then presented, including perturbations from solar radiation pressure, solar J2 oblateness, Jupiter third-body gravity, and relativistic effects. Finally, the method is applied to a multiple-revolution Earth-Venus transfer under high-fidelity dynamics.

Abstract: This paper studies difference-of-convex (DC) optimization problems through smoothing descent techniques. In particular, we introduce the difference of high-order Moreau envelopes (HOME-DC) and establish its fundamental and differential properties. Approximating the underlying proximal points, we generate an inexact first-order oracle for HOME-DC and characterize its accuracy guarantees. Building upon this oracle, we propose a class of inexact descent methods for minimizing DC functions and provide a convergence analysis. The proposed framework extends the applicability of envelope-based optimization techniques to a broad class of structured nonconvex problems while accommodating inexact solutions to subproblems. Preliminary numerical experiments on a sparse clustering problem demonstrate the approach's practical potential and support the theoretical findings.

Abstract: Each year, disruptions in the air transportation network strand millions of passengers and cost airlines billions in revenue. Airline networks prioritize operational and cost efficiency through hub-and-spoke structures that maximize revenue; however, these hubs also act as critical choke points during disruptions. Previous studies have focused on reactionary measures in response to air transportation network disruptions, whereas this work proposes a proactive strategy to improve resilience by reconfiguring the network's topology. Specifically, we consider airside-to-airside bus lines as a low-cost, frequent alternative to short, regional flights, offering service that can circumvent air traffic-related delays. We develop a network construction model that augments the existing air transportation network with these bus lines. The augmented networks are analyzed through an agent-based simulation, where increased resilience is measured in terms of decreased average hourly passenger delays under both nominal and disrupted conditions. Our results demonstrate that converting 10 regional routes from air service to airside-to-airside bus service, for a baseline scenario that is constrained by a $10 million investment budget, can reduce passenger delays by an average of 8% on disrupted days and 6% on nominal days. Furthermore, through a sensitivity analysis, we show that while augmenting the system using these buses decreases operational costs compared to the historic air-only network, continuously expanding bus parameters (i.e., range and investment budget) yields diminishing returns in delay mitigation. Finally, we discuss real-world precedents alongside regulatory and political hurdles to implementation. The proposed framework offers airlines, airports, and regulators a decision-support tool for integrating multimodal strategies into future disruption management policies.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出方向-幅度分解（DMD）统一框架，将优化变量分解为方向分量和幅度分量。基于此开发两种方法：(1) 过参数化DMD：使用大于所需的秩r，且收敛速度随r增加而加快；(2) 递归DMD：受过参数化DMD中增量特征学习（鞍点-到-鞍点行为）启发，以更低的内存和计算成本实现互补。

Result: 两种DMD方法在矩阵分解问题上的收敛速度均比BM公式下的梯度下降指数级更快。矩阵分解、感知和补全等数值实验验证了理论发现和实际有效性。

Conclusion: DMD框架为低秩矩阵优化提供了一种高效且秩选择鲁棒的替代方案，过参数化DMD和递归DMD分别在计算速度和资源效率上各具优势，两者互补。

Abstract: Low-rank matrix optimization is often carried out via the Burer-Monteiro (BM) formulation, but choosing the factorization rank $r$ is delicate and can substantially slow optimization. We propose a unified framework, termed direction-magnitude decomposition (DMD), that decomposes the optimization variable to improve optimization efficiency even when the target rank is unknown. We develop two DMD-based approaches and establish their theoretical advantages on the canonical problem of matrix factorization. The first, overparameterized DMD, uses a rank $r$ larger than necessary and enjoys faster convergence as $r$ increases. The second, recursive DMD, is motivated by the incremental eigenpair learning, or saddle-to-saddle, behavior of overparameterized DMD. It achieves lower memory and computational costs, complementing overparameterized DMD. Both approaches are exponentially faster than gradient descent applied to the BM formulation. Numerical experiments on matrix factorization, sensing, and completion corroborate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of DMD.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes a modular vehicle system for passenger-freight integration along a bidirectional transit corridor. The system uses homogeneous units that can be coupled into vehicles and assigned to either passenger or freight service. Freight is carried by dedicated units, with loading and unloading coordinated with docking and undocking and separated from passenger boarding and alighting. To better respond to uncertain passenger demand and integrate freight transport, vehicles can be reconfigured at intermediate stations, where they can also depart and terminate. We jointly optimize departure-specific service routes, timetables, vehicle compositions, unit schedules, and passenger-freight demand assignments, with unit reuse constrained by explicit docking and undocking times. These decisions are modeled on a space-time-state network and formulated as a stochastic mixed-integer program that minimizes unit deployment costs, passenger waiting costs, and penalties for unmet freight demand. Passenger demand uncertainty is addressed using linearized chance constraints. To solve the problem, we develop an exact Benders decomposition algorithm with valid inequalities and a warm-start strategy, together with a tailored decomposition-based heuristic for larger instances. Computational experiments on instances generated from representative transit corridors in Gothenburg demonstrate the effectiveness of the Benders algorithm for small- and medium-sized instances and the scalability of the heuristic for larger problems. Sensitivity analyses highlight the value of accounting for passenger demand uncertainty and the effects of temporal overlap between passenger and freight demand. Comparisons with benchmark transit systems further demonstrate the operational advantages of the proposed modular integrated system.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the rate of convergence of the relative value iteration (RVI) algorithms for diffusions in $\mathbb{R}^d$ under both the conventional ergodic cost (CEC) and ergodic risk-sensitive cost (ERSC) criteria, and under the uniform exponential stability condition. The existing RVI algorithms for the CEC and ERSC problems solve the associated initial value Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman type equations whose solutions are shown to converge asymptotically to the corresponding optimal values. However, the rates of convergence for such algorithms have remained open. This paper proposes discrete-time implementations for the RVI algorithms based on slight modifications of the associated PDEs, and proves that the rates of convergence of these RVI algorithms are exponential under a weighted sup-norm. These implementations have discrete-time iterates that can be explicitly expressed as recursive systems. The difference between these iterates and the desired value function in the CEC case can then be expressed in terms of the associated Markov kernels. Similarly, this can be done for the logarithms of the corresponding iterates and desired value function in the ERSC case in terms of the associated Markov kernels for the extended diffusion. As a result, we are able to prove the desirable contraction properties in order to establish the exponential rate of convergence by making use of a weighted semi-norm in which Markov kernel acts a contraction.

</details>


### [68] [ILPU: Iterative Laplace-Based Phase Unwrapping via Bi-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31460)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位解缠是磁共振相位成像应用（如磁敏感性成像、场图映射、温度测量和MR弹性成像）中的关键预处理步骤，现有方法在低信噪比区域和三维体积解缠中性能不足，需要一种既鲁棒又计算高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出双层优化算法ILPU：下层求解器通过离散余弦变换求解增量泊松方程恢复连续相位增量；上层求解器通过质量引导的空间正则化和受限局部搜索优化整数偏移图，引入自适应平滑惩罚和质量加权正则化以增强低信噪比区域的鲁棒性。

Result: 在2D脑部MRI相位图像上精度与SEGUE相当；在3D体积解缠中，ILPU相对误差为2.12%，而SEGUE为67.59%，Laplace为81.02%。算法计算复杂度为O(N log N)，数值速度优于Flynn和SEGUE。

Conclusion: 双层优化框架为MRI相位解缠提供了一种鲁棒且计算高效的解决方案，在三维体积解缠中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Phase unwrapping is an essential preprocessing step for phase-based MRI applications, including susceptibility mapping, field mapping, thermometry, and MR elastography. We present Iterative Laplace-Based Phase Unwrapping (ILPU), a bi-level optimization algorithm. In this method, a lower-level solver recovers a continuous phase increment from an incremental Poisson equation using the discrete cosine transform (DCT), while an upper-level solver refines an integer offset map through quality-guided spatial regularization and a restricted local search.
  This coupling enables robust unwrapping in low-SNR regions through adaptive smoothness penalties and quality-weighted regularization. We evaluated ILPU on 2D and 3D brain MRI phase images against manually unwrapped reference data, using standard Laplace unwrapping, Flynn, and SEGUE as comparison methods. In 2D, ILPU achieves accuracy comparable to SEGUE. In 3D, ILPU attains a relative error of 2.12% compared with 67.59% for SEGUE and 81.02% for Laplace, demonstrating a clear advantage in volumetric unwrapping.
  The algorithm has O(N log N) complexity per iteration through DCT-based Laplacian estimation and is numerically faster than both Flynn and SEGUE while preserving superior accuracy. These results indicate that the bi-level optimization framework provides a robust and computationally efficient solution for phase unwrapping in MRI.

</details>


### [69] [A Geometry-Adaptive Regularized Newton-Type Method for Manifold-Affine Intersection Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31738)
*Dengyu Zheng,Shixiang Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化牛顿-SLRA (RN-SLRA) 算法，通过引入正则化项解决流形-仿射子空间交问题中横截性条件失效时的算法定义性问题，证明了在内蕴横截性条件下的线性收敛性和横截性条件下的高阶（含二次）收敛性，并分析了不精确投影变体的收敛性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Newton-SLRA 算法在解决流形-仿射子空间交问题（如结构化低秩逼近）时高效，但其良好定义性依赖于流形与仿射子空间之间的横截性条件，该条件在实际问题中可能不成立，导致算法失效。本文旨在克服这一困难。

Method: 引入正则化项，构造 Regularized Newton-SLRA (RN-SLRA) 算法，解决了原算法在横截性条件不满足时的定义性问题。同时研究了不精确投影变体，即允许对流形的投影近似计算，并分析了收敛性质。

Result: 证明了在内蕴横截性条件下 RN-SLRA 线性收敛；在横截性条件下达到高阶收敛（正则化参数合适时可达二次收敛）。不精确投影变体在相应假设下保持相同的局部线性和二次收敛性质。数值实验（包括构造退化实例和 Hankel 结构例子）表明 RN-SLRA 在 Newton-SLRA 可能失效的场景下鲁棒性显著提升。

Conclusion: RN-SLRA 有效克服了弱交条件下横截性假设不满足导致的算法定义性问题，保留了高阶收敛速率的同时显著提升了鲁棒性，为流形-仿射子空间交问题提供了更可靠的计算框架。

Abstract: We propose a regularized algorithm, Regularized Newton-SLRA (RN-SLRA), for local manifold--affine intersection problems under weak intersection conditions, motivated in particular by structured low-rank approximation (SLRA). Newton-SLRA is an efficient method for manifold--affine intersection problems, but its well-definedness relies on the transversality condition between the manifold and the affine subspace, a condition that may fail in practice. RN-SLRA overcomes this difficulty by introducing a regularization term. We prove that, under the intrinsic transversality condition, RN-SLRA converges linearly to the intersection, while under the transversality condition it achieves higher-order convergence, including quadratic convergence for a suitable choice of the regularization parameter. We also study an inexact-projection variant, in which the projection onto the manifold is computed approximately, and show that the same local linear and quadratic convergence properties are preserved under the corresponding assumptions. Numerical experiments on constructed degenerate instances and Hankel-structured examples illustrate improved robustness in settings where Newton-SLRA may fail.

</details>


### [70] [Explicit Series and a Certified Hybrid Evaluator for the $\ell_p$ Proximity Operator for $0<p<1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31951)
*Lixin Shen,Jiangyu Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸ℓ_p拟范数(0<p<1)的邻近算子计算难题，通过Lagrange–Bürmann反演给出了阈值以上的精确级数解，并利用Mellin–Barnes积分表示设计了带可计算先验误差界的混合评估器；对于有理数p，借助Gauss乘法定理将级数简化为广义超几何函数的有限和，解释了p∈{1/3,1/2,2/3}处闭式解的来源。将评估器集成到非精确邻近梯度法并证明收敛性，数值实验验证了精度，尤其在近阈值区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸ℓ_p拟范数(0<p<1)是比ℓ1范数更强大的稀疏性代理，但其邻近算子的稳健求解极具挑战性，现有方法在阈值附近存在数值不稳定问题。

Method: (1) 利用Lagrange–Bürmann反演推导标量邻近映射的均匀收敛级数，给出精确阈值结构；(2) 建立Mellin–Barnes积分表示并分析收敛半径，实现可认证截断；(3) 设计短级数+垂直MB截断段的混合评估器，附带可计算先验误差界；(4) 对有理数p，用Gauss乘法定理将系数简化为Gamma函数的有限积，级数重写为广义超几何函数的有限和。

Result: 给出了标量邻近映射在所有0<p<1范围内的显式表征，包括阈值结构和严格孤立解的条件；混合评估器在近阈值区域保持高精度；对于有理数p获得了封闭形式的解析表达式；集成到非精确邻近梯度法后证明了收敛性，数值实验验证了准确性。

Conclusion: 该工作为ℓ_p拟范数邻近算子提供了首个兼具理论精确性和数值稳定性的通用计算框架，解决了长期存在的近阈值数值不稳定问题，并为稀疏优化中非凸正则化方法提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: The nonconvex $\ell_p$ quasi-norm with $0<p<1$ is a powerful sparsity surrogate but makes the proximity operator $\mathrm{prox}_{λ|\cdot|^p}$ nontrivial to evaluate robustly. We give an explicit characterization of the scalar proximal map for all $0<p<1$, including the threshold structure and conditions ensuring strict, isolated solutions. Applying the Lagrange--Bürmann inversion to the stationarity equation yields a uniformly convergent series for the larger positive root, which provides an exact and numerically stable formula above the classical threshold. We further derive a Mellin--Barnes (MB) integral representation, explaining its radius of convergence and enabling certified truncation. Building on these ingredients, we design a {certified hybrid evaluator} (short series $+$ truncated vertical MB segment) with a computable a priori error bound that remains accurate in the near-threshold regime. For rational $p$, Gauss' multiplication formula reduces the coefficients to finite products of shifted Gamma functions, reorganizing the series into a finite sum of generalized hypergeometric functions and explaining the closed forms at $p\in\{1/3,1/2,2/3\}$. We integrate the evaluator into a proximal-gradient method with an inexact proximal oracle and prove convergence under standard summability of the certificates; MATLAB implementations and numerics confirm accuracy, including near-threshold behavior.

</details>


### [71] [Approximate Controllability of the generalized Burgers-Huxley equation in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31968)
*Aman Patel,Mohmedmunavvar Mubarak Bapu,Mrinmay Biswas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义Burgers-Huxley（GBH）方程在内点控制下的可控性。结果表明该方程在给定时间内不是全局近似可控的，但可以通过局部内点控制在适当时间内将系统从一个稳态驱动到另一稳态的任意小邻域内，前提是两个稳态位于稳态集的同一连通分支中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBH方程是描述反应、对流和扩散相互作用的原型模型，探索其可控性对于理解此类非线性偏微分方程系统的控制能力具有重要理论意义。

Method: 采用内点控制方法，控制支撑在定义域内任意非空开子集上；通过分析稳态集的连通性结构来刻画可控性条件。

Result: (1) GBH方程在给定时间内不是全局近似可控的；(2) 在一定条件下可实现局部可控性：可将系统从一个稳态驱动到同一连通分支内另一稳态的任意小邻域。

Conclusion: GBH方程的可控性受限于稳态集的拓扑结构，全局可控性不成立，但在同一连通分支内可实现局部近似可控，这为非线性反应-对流-扩散系统的控制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The generalized Burgers-Huxley (GBH) equation is a prototype model that describes the interplay among reaction, convection, and diffusion. In this article, we explore the controllability of this model by means of an interior control supported in an arbitrary non-empty open subset of the domain. We establish that the GBH equation is not globally approximately controllable in a given time. However, it is possible to steer the system from any steady state to an arbitrarily small neighborhood of another steady state in some suitable time by means of a localized interior control, provided that both steady states lie in the same connected component of the set of steady states.

</details>


### [72] [Random Reshuffling Dominates Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32005)
*Zijian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了在光滑凸优化中，无论采用何种合理步长，经过任意有限轮迭代，随机重排（RR）策略在随机洗牌SGD中的表现始终优于传统的SGD，解决了长期存在的理论开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管随机重排（RR）在实际应用中表现优异，但其收敛理论存在两个关键限制：(1) 步长必须小于与1/n成正比的阈值；(2) 当迭代轮数小于与n成正比的阈值时，RR的理论最优速率严格劣于SGD。这些限制严重削弱了现有理论的适用性，与实际观察严重不符。

Method: 通过理论分析和数学证明，在光滑凸优化框架下，对RR与SGD在任意合理步长和任意有限轮迭代下的收敛性能进行严格比较。

Result: 首次证明了在光滑凸优化中，RR在任意合理步长和任意有限轮迭代下始终优于SGD，消除了现有理论中步长和迭代轮数的限制条件。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了RR收敛理论中的一个长期开放问题，弥合了理论与实践之间的差距，为RR的优越性提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\textsf{SGD}$) is one of the most classical optimization algorithms with favorable theoretical guarantees, yet the practical implementation of $\textsf{SGD}$ differs subtly from its well-known form and is often referred to as Shuffling Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$). A particularly popular strategy in $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ is Random Reshuffling ($\textsf{RR}$), which has achieved great empirical success across numerous experiments. Despite its strong performance, $\textsf{RR}$ has long been considered a heuristic due to a lack of theoretical support. Over the last decade, people have finally established provable convergence rates for $\textsf{RR}$, thus justifying its observed superiority. However, for smooth convex optimization, two clouds over the convergence theory of $\textsf{RR}$ remain to this day. More precisely, according to the current theory, $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ under $\textsf{RR}$ converges only when the stepsize is smaller than a threshold proportional to $1/n$, where $n$ is the number of summands in the objective (or the number of data points). Consequently, the optimally tuned theoretical rate of $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ under $\textsf{RR}$ is strictly worse than that of $\textsf{SGD}$ when the number of epochs is smaller than another threshold proportional to $n$. These two restrictions heavily limit the applicability of existing theories and leave a critical mismatch with practice. In this work, for the first time, we prove that $\textsf{RR}$ dominates $\textsf{SGD}$ in smooth convex optimization under any reasonable stepsize after any finite number of epochs, thereby addressing a longstanding open question.

</details>

### [73] [Joint discovery of governing partial differential equations from multi-source datasets by competitive optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30699)
*Hao Xu,Siyu Lou,Yuntian Chen,Dongxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCO-PDE框架，通过软竞争加权机制融合多源数据，从观测数据中自动发现控制方程（偏微分方程），在少量数据（每数据集50个观测点）下即可高精度恢复经典方程，支持二维/三维不规则域和非均匀系数，并在真实波浪实验数据中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法通常基于单一数据集发现控制方程，当观测受限时性能有限；而实际中同一物理系统常存在仅初始/边界条件不同的多源数据集，如何有效融合这些数据以提升方程发现能力是核心动机。

Method: 提出MCO-PDE框架：1) 为每个数据源独立训练神经代理模型；2) 采用软竞争加权机制动态评估各数据源可信度并聚合全局共识系数；3) 结合遗传算法进行结构搜索，同时识别控制方程的函数形式和参数。

Result: 在7个案例中，每数据集仅50个观测点即可高精度恢复经典方程；框架能处理二维/三维不规则边界和非均匀系数场景；在真实波浪水槽实验中成功提取出符合物理意义的控制定律。

Conclusion: 该工作通过异构数据融合为自动化科学发现提供了一条有前景的途径。

Abstract: Discovering governing equations directly from observational data is a key step towards interpretable scientific machine learning. Current data-driven approaches typically operate on a single dataset, inherently limiting their performance when faced with restricted observations. In practice, multiple datasets are often available for the same physical system, distinguished only by distinct initial conditions or boundary configurations. Here, we present a competitive optimization framework designed to discover shared partial differential equations (PDEs) from multi-source datasets, termed MCO-PDE. The framework first trains independent neural surrogates for each data source, and then employs a soft-competitive weighting mechanism to dynamically assess dataset credibility and aggregate a consensus global coefficient. Integrated with a genetic algorithm for structural search, this approach simultaneously identifies the functional forms and parameters of the governing laws. We demonstrate that fusing as few as 50 observations per dataset across seven cases recovers canonical equations with high accuracy. The framework inherently handles two- and three-dimensional domains characterized by irregular boundaries and heterogeneous coefficients, and successfully extracts physically meaningful laws from real-world wave-tank experiments. Overall, this work establishes a promising route for automated scientific discovery via heterogeneous data fusion.

</details>


### [74] [Accelerometry-Derived Digital Biomarkers for Cardiometabolic Risk: A Population-Representative Tabular Benchmark with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30702)
*Federico Felizzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于NHANES 2003-2006数据构建了加速度计心脏代谢基准，评估了三种表格学习方法（岭回归、XGBoost、TabPFN v2）预测HbA1c、甘油三酯和CRP的性能，并应用分裂共形预测评估了人口亚组的覆盖公平性。TabPFN v2表现最佳，但甘油三酯预测困难，且存在亚组覆盖不足的公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能反映真实世界临床表格数据的特性（如复杂抽样、人口过度采样和亚组公平性），亟需一个能体现这些特性的基准来评估表格学习方法在临床环境中的实际表现。

Method: 使用NHANES 2003-2006数据（1381名成人），采用三种表格学习方法（岭回归、XGBoost、TabPFN v2）预测糖化血红蛋白、空腹甘油三酯和C反应蛋白，并应用分裂共形预测生成90%预测区间，评估性别和种族/民族亚组的覆盖公平性。

Result: TabPFN v2表现最优（HbA1c R²=0.156，CRP R²=0.383）；甘油三酯几乎不可预测（R²<0.05）；边际覆盖在CRP和HbA1c上接近90%目标但甘油三酯不达标；亚组层面存在局部覆盖不足（如墨西哥裔美国人的HbA1c）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了边际保证与临床公平所需条件覆盖之间的差距，强调亚组公平性评估的重要性，并提供了公开可复现的代码和数据。

Abstract: Structured tabular data dominates clinical medicine, yet existing benchmarks fail to reflect real-world properties like complex survey sampling, demographic oversampling, and subgroup fairness. We introduce the NHANES Accelerometry Cardiometabolic Benchmark, derived from NHANES 2003-2006, comprising 1,381 adults with hip-worn accelerometry, fasting laboratory biomarkers, dietary intake, and anthropometrics. We evaluate three tabular learning methods -- ridge regression, XGBoost, and the foundation model TabPFN v2 -- to predict glycated haemoglobin (HbA1c), fasting triglycerides, and C-reactive protein (CRP) from activity phenotypes and lifestyle covariates. TabPFN v2 achieves the best overall performance (HbA1c R^2=0.156, CRP R^2=0.383), while triglycerides remain largely unpredictable (R^2 < 0.05), consistent with known genetic dominance. We apply split conformal prediction to generate distribution-free 90% prediction intervals and evaluate demographic coverage equity across sex and race/ethnicity subgroups. Marginal coverage aligns with the 90% target for CRP and HbA1c but falls below for triglycerides. At the subgroup level, we observe localized undercoverage (e.g., HbA1c for Mexican American participants), illustrating the gap between marginal guarantees and the conditional coverage required for clinical fairness. Code and data are at https://github.com/felizzi/nhanes-accel-cardiometabolic-benchmark.

</details>


### [75] [From Search to Synthesis: Training LLMs as Zero-Shot Workflow Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30704)
*Gan Luo,Zihan Qin,Bin Dong,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaFlow将工作流生成视为元学习问题，通过两阶段训练（监督微调+强化学习）生成可跨任务泛化的算法工作流，在问答、代码生成和数学推理基准上达到SOTA水平，并展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的实例级解决方案缺乏结构一致性，手动设计任务级工作流需要大量专业知识和努力，现有方法要么产生实例级方案而无法学习任务级模式，要么无法泛化到训练配置之外，因此需要一种自动的工作流生成方法。

Method: MetaFlow将工作流生成建模为元学习问题，分两阶段训练：首先在合成工作流数据上进行监督微调，然后使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），利用任务内多个问题实例的执行反馈来优化端到端成功率。

Result: 在问答、代码生成和数学推理基准上，MetaFlow在领域内任务上通过单次推理达到与SOTA基线相当的性能，同时在领域外任务和操作集上展现出显著的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaFlow通过元学习框架有效实现了自动工作流生成，不仅对训练过的任务有效，还能强泛化到未训练的任务和新操作集，解决了工作流自动生成的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel across a wide range of tasks, yet their instance-specific solutions often lack the structural consistency needed for reliable deployment. Workflows that encode recurring algorithmic patterns at the task level provide a principled framework, offering robustness across instance variations, interpretable traces for debugging, and reusability across problem instances. However, manually designing such workflows requires significant expertise and effort, limiting their broader application. While automatic workflow generation could address this bottleneck, existing methods either produce instance-specific solutions without learning task-level patterns, or cannot generalize beyond their training configurations. We present MetaFlow, which casts workflow generation as a meta-learning problem: given a task and an operator set, the model learns to compose solution strategies. MetaFlow trains in two stages: supervised fine-tuning on synthetic workflow data, followed by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) that uses execution feedback across problem instances in the task to improve end-to-end success. The resulting model produces effective workflows for trained tasks and exhibits strong generalization to untrained tasks and novel operator sets. Across benchmarks in question answering, code generation, and mathematical reasoning, MetaFlow achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art baselines on in-domain tasks with single inference, while demonstrating remarkable zero-shot generalization capabilities on out-of-domain tasks and operator sets.

</details>


### [76] [Why Do Few-Step Text Latents Fail When Image Latents Work? Non-Commitment at Sharp Categorical Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30705)
*Zhongyao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了确定性连续潜变量模型在文本生成中失效的根本原因是几何上的解码器尖锐性而非训练不足，并提出了两种逃逸机制（自回归编码器和随机重注入）。通过理论证明和诊断指标（DABI和CCI）分析了连续文本解码器的分支选择失败问题，并建立了精度-深度-刚度权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释确定性几步生成在连续文本潜变量上崩溃为不连贯文本的原因，证明这是几何因素（解码器尖锐性）而非训练或缩放不足导致的。

Method: 通过理论证明（Theorem 3）建立后验均值终态步骤在决策边界附近的令牌翻转率；提出两个诊断指标DABI（读出尖锐性）和CCI（分类承诺度）；在理想化分离机制中证明匹配的尖锐传输定律和维度相图。

Result: 四个独立构建的连续文本解码器的DABI从5×10²到>10⁵，而图像解码器的DABI≈1。确定性ODE在K=4时PPL为294，而SDE为50。分离机制中确定性刚度与模式数M的关系为Θ(√log M)（当潜维度Ω(log M)时）或M^{1/n}（固定维度时）。

Conclusion: 确定性连续类内部的成本是不可约的，两种逃逸机制（分类承诺和随机重注入）均需跳出该框架。建立了精度-深度-刚度权衡关系：深度B的层级结构使每步峰值减小√B倍。

Abstract: Deterministic few-step generation succeeds on continuous image latents but collapses to incoherent text on continuous text latents, and we show the cause is geometric rather than a training or scaling deficiency: a smooth, regularity-limited deterministic map cannot resolve a discrete branch choice before a sharp categorical readout, so few-step failure is governed by decoder sharpness, not transport accuracy. In the overlapping regime of real text autoencoders, we prove (Theorem 3) that the posterior-mean terminal step flips tokens at the rate of the latent mass in an $O(s(t))$ tube around decision boundaries.
