Conclusion: 数据集大小是影响体积假说验证结果的关键因素，该发现调和了此前实验矛盾，支持了体积假说在充足数据条件下的合理性。

Abstract: Modern deep neural networks often contain far more parameters than needed to fit their training data, yet they achieve impressive generalization. A common explanation for this success is the implicit bias of stochastic gradient descent (SGD). An alternative volume hypothesis posits that, within low training-loss regions, loss-landscape basins leading to strong generalization occupy much larger regions of weight space than basins that generalize poorly, and therefore SGD is simply more likely to land in the former. Recent experimental explorations of this idea present seemingly contradictory results. While in one set of experiments randomly sampling the network weights until achieving zero training error yielded poor generalization, molecular dynamics density estimates supported the volume hypothesis. We observe that these experiments were performed at different dataset size regimes, and explore an intermediate regime using the Replica Exchange Wang-Landau algorithm to estimate the joint density of states over training and test accuracies in binary networks. Across several architectures and datasets, we show that the generalization advantage of gradient learning over random sampling training generally diminishes as the training data size grows, suggesting a resolution of the paradox.

</details>


### [113] [Sequential sparse Gaussian process quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31284)
*Hugo Nicolas,Olivier Le Maître*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于稀疏高斯过程与拉普拉斯近斯的贝叶斯分位数回归框架，通过将预测不确定性分解为条件先验与后验方差分量，驱动诱导点填充与数据采集两种自适应机制，在保证计算可行性的同时实现模型复杂度与预测精度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯高斯过程分位数回归虽能提供不确定性量化，但非共轭的非对称拉普拉斯似然导致后验推断计算成本极高，亟需一种兼顾计算效率与预测精度的可扩展方法。

Method: 采用稀疏高斯过程，通过诱导变量子集表示分位数函数；使用拉普拉斯近似进行后验推断；将预测不确定性分解为条件先验方差与后验方差分量，据此设计诱导点填充与数据采集两种自适应机制，并构建序贯算法动态分配计算资源。

Result: 在基准问题上验证了拉普拉斯近似的准确性、基于方差的诱导点放置策略的优势，以及所提序贯充实策略相较预定数据采集策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够以可控的计算成本实现高效的不确定性量化，自适应地平衡模型复杂度与预测精度，为大规模分位数回归提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Quantile regression aims to estimate the conditional quantiles of a response variable from observed data. In a Bayesian setting, Gaussian process quantile regression provides uncertainty quantification but faces significant computational challenges due to the nonconjugacy of the asymmetric Laplace likelihood and the cost of posterior inference. We develop a sparse Gaussian process framework in which the quantile function is represented through a reduced set of inducing variables and posterior inference is performed using a Laplace approximation. A decomposition of the predictive uncertainty into conditional-prior and posterior-induced variance components is then exploited to drive two complementary adaptive mechanisms: inducing-input infilling and data acquisition. These mechanisms are combined within a sequential algorithm that allocates computational effort toward the dominant source of predictive uncertainty and adaptively controls model complexity. Numerical experiments on benchmark problems demonstrate the accuracy of the Laplace approximation, the benefits of variance-based inducing-input placement, and the effectiveness of the proposed sequential enrichment strategy compared with predefined data-acquisition strategies.

</details>


### [114] [Probabilistic Inversion with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31288)
*Baldur Paulwitz,Stefan Buske*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了将生成式人工智能中的Flow Matching技术应用于地球物理概率反演（如地震全波形反演），并在简单2D速度模型和复杂OpenFWI数据集上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将生成式AI领域的Flow Matching理论适配到地球物理概率反演场景，以解决传统反演方法在不确定性量化方面的不足。

Method: 将Flow Matching的数学理论从生成式AI适配到概率反演框架，并在简单2D速度模型和OpenFWI复杂数据集上进行了案例研究。

Result: 在简单2D模型上验证了方法的基本特征，在OpenFWI数据集上展示了其对复杂地震速度模型进行概率反演的能力。

Conclusion: Flow Matching作为一种新兴的生成式AI技术，可以有效应用于地球物理概率反演，并具备处理复杂模型的能力。

Abstract: We demonstrate the application of Flow Matching, a technique originating from generative Artificial Intelligence, to probabilistic inversion in geophysical settings, such as seismic Full-Waveform inversion. We adapt the well-established mathematical theory of Flow Matching from generative Artificial Intelligence to the context of probabilistic inversion. We evaluate the approach with two case studies: a simple 2D velocity model to illustrate the general features of the method, and the OpenFWI dataset to show its capabilities for probabilistic inversion of more complex seismic velocity models.

</details>


### [115] [Patch-PODiff-ViT: Structured Latent Diffusion with Patchwise POD for Super-Resolution and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31290)
*Onkar Jadhav,Tim French,Matthew Rayson,Nicole L. Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Patch-PODiff-ViT框架，用基于局部补丁的Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD)固定线性正交基替代非线性自编码器构建潜空间，实现高效低维扩散；因解码器为固定线性正交，可解析传播潜系数不确定性到物理空间预测方差，无需蒙特卡洛估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型计算昂贵，而现有学习的潜空间缺乏可解释的不确定性量化。

Method: 利用Patchwise Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD)构建固定线性正交基作为潜空间，结合Vision Transformer进行结构化低维潜扩散；因解码器线性且正交，潜系数不确定性可解析传播到物理空间预测方差。

Result: 在海表温度、医学成像和自然图像上，该方法以更少参数和更低内存实现强重建，并产生与经验集成高度匹配的校准良好的空间不确定性。

Conclusion: 固定线性正交潜空间结合Transformer扩散可同时实现计算效率提升与可解释不确定性量化，为概率超分辨率提供新路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models enable probabilistic super-resolution and conditional generation, but pixel-space methods are computationally expensive and learned latent spaces often lack interpretable uncertainty quantification. We introduce Patch-PODiff-ViT, a structured latent diffusion framework in which the latent space is defined by patchwise Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD), a fixed linear orthonormal basis over local patches, rather than learned by a nonlinear autoencoder. This yields low-dimensional, variance-ordered tokens that preserve spatial structure and enable efficient diffusion in a structured low-dimensional latent space with a Vision Transformer. Because the decoder is fixed, linear, and orthonormal, latent coefficient uncertainty can be propagated directly to physical-space predictive variance, enabling analytic propagation of predictive variance through the linear decoder without Monte Carlo estimation in pixel space. Across sea surface temperature, medical imaging, and natural images, the method achieves strong reconstruction with fewer parameters and lower memory, while producing well-calibrated spatial uncertainty that closely matches empirical ensembles.

</details>


### [116] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Spacecraft Attitude Control During Atmospheric Re-Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31291)
*Alexander Fabisch,Melvin Laux,Mariela De Lucas Álvarez,Edoardo Caroselli,Julian Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了深度强化学习在航天器再入大气层姿态控制中的应用，提出混合控制器（RL+传统PID增益调度）方法。通过动态随机化训练增强泛化能力，实验表明混合控制器在攻角跟踪和鲁棒性（质量、惯性张量、襟翼执行器带宽变化）上优于传统控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统姿态控制方法在处理非线性动力学、不确定性和故障情况时存在局限，深度强化学习有望提供更自适应、精确和鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 以带增益调度的工业标准PID控制器作为基线，应用连续离策略强化学习（SOTA RL），并结合动态随机化进行训练以增强在预定操作包线内的泛化能力。

Result: SOTA RL在域内性能与传统控制方法相当，但分布外泛化不足；混合控制器在操作包线内的攻角跟踪和鲁棒性（质量、惯性张量、襟翼执行器带宽变化）上表现更优。

Conclusion: 混合控制器（RL+传统控制）在航天器再入姿态控制中具有优越性能，尤其在对系统参数变化和不确定性的鲁棒性方面优于纯传统方法。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has the potential to solve attitude control problems more adaptively, precisely, and robustly by handling nonlinear dynamics, uncertainties, and failure cases more effectively than traditional attitude control approaches. We explore reinforcement learning (RL) for attitude control in spacecraft re-entry. An industry-standard proportional-integral-derivative controller with gain scheduling serves as a strong baseline for model-free RL and hybrid controllers that combine these two approaches. We formalize the application in the RL framework to apply continuous, off-policy RL. State-of-the-art RL achieves comparable performance to traditional control approaches in this domain. However, its out-of-distribution generalization is not sufficient. Hence, we use dynamics randomization to introduce challenging task variations during training and enforce generalization in a predefined operational envelope. Finally, we assess the best obtained RL-based controllers with application-specific metrics to show superior performance in comparison to traditional controllers in the operational envelope, that is, hybrid controllers are able to track the angle of attack better and are more robust under variations of mass, inertia tensor, and flap actuator bandwidth.

</details>


### [117] [Safe Online Learning via Smooth Safety-Structured Policy Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31320)
*Hongpeng Cao,Liqun Zhao,Yuliang Gu,Naira Hovakimyan,Lui Sha,Marco Caccamo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSafe是一种安全感知的策略架构，通过在动作生成过程中集成结构化的安全监控与干预机制，实现性能驱动和安全保持行为之间的平滑、风险依赖转换，在保证安全性的同时维持连续的在线学习动态，并在连续控制基准和物理倒立摆系统上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临两难：严格的安全执行（如动作干预）会破坏系统交互与学习的连续性；而软安全约束虽能保持平滑学习但安全保障有限。需要一种既能保证安全又不牺牲学习平滑性的方法。

Method: 提出AutoSafe安全感知策略架构，将结构化安全监控与干预直接集成到动作生成过程中，实现性能驱动行为与安全保持行为之间的平滑、风险依赖转换，从而维持连续的在线交互与学习动态。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试中展示了强大的安全执行能力且不牺牲学习平滑性，并在物理倒立摆系统上验证了其在实际环境中的安全在线学习有效性。

Conclusion: AutoSafe能够在保障安全性的同时维持平滑的学习动态，为现实世界的安全在线强化学习提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Safe online reinforcement learning requires policies to respect safety constraints while maintaining smooth optimization dynamics. Existing approaches typically rely on either strict safety enforcement via action interventions, which introduce discontinuities in system interaction and learning, or soft safety constraint formulations, which preserve smooth learning but provide limited safety assurance. We propose AutoSafe, a safety-aware policy architecture that integrates structured safety monitoring and intervention directly into the action generation process. This design enables smooth, risk-dependent transitions between performance-driven and safety-preserving behaviors, resulting in continuous online interaction and learning dynamics. Empirical results across a suite of continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate strong safety enforcement without sacrificing learning smoothness. We further validate AutoSafe on a physical cart-pole system, highlighting its practical effectiveness for safe online learning in the real world.

</details>


### [118] [Expected Gain-based Escalation in Vertical Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31331)
*Mohamad Mestoukirdi,Vincent Corlay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种选择性升级机制，在垂直联邦学习的两轮推理协议中，仅在预期能提升最终决策时才调用高成本的嵌入融合轮次，通过基于期望增益的评分估计来优化通信-准确率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在垂直联邦学习中，对每个样本都进行协作融合会带来不必要的通信和计算开销，需要一种选择性策略来在通信成本与预测精度之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出两轮推理协议：第一轮低成本推理基于客户端后验生成预测；第二轮嵌入融合仅在预期能改善决策时被调用。路由决策基于期望增益评分估计，该评分结合了校准的池化后验与VFL模型的类别级可靠性估计，均从保留的校准数据中获得，无需单独训练路由网络。

Result: 在多视图分类基准测试及受控测试时视图退化的场景下，所提出的路由器在通信-准确率权衡上优于基于置信度、学习增益和延迟的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过可解释的评分机制实现选择性升级，能够有效减少不必要的通信开销，同时保持或提升预测性能，为VFL中的高效推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Collaborative inference can improve predictive performance by integrating complementary information across agents, but applying collaborative fusion to every sample can incur unnecessary communication and computational overhead. This trade-off is particularly relevant in vertical federated learning (VFL), where clients observe different views of the same sample and fusion typically requires transmitting intermediate representations to a server. We study selective escalation in a two-round VFL inference protocol, in which a low-cost first round produces a prediction from client posteriors and a second embedding-fusion round is invoked only when it is expected to improve the final decision. We formulate routing as expected-gain score estimation: a sample is escalated when a predicted improvement in correctness justifies the additional communication. The proposed analytical score combines a calibrated pooled posterior with classwise reliability estimates of the VFL model, both obtained from held-out calibration data, yielding an interpretable router that requires no separately trained routing network. Experiments on multi-view classification benchmarks, including controlled test--time view degradation settings, show that the proposed router improves the communication-accuracy trade-off over confidence-, learned-gain-, and deferral-based baselines.

</details>


### [119] [Dualformer: Efficient Feature Extractor for Complex-valued Blind Communication Signal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31352)
*Yurui Zhao,Xiang Wang,Jingreng Lei,Wanlong Zhang,Yik-Chung Wu,Zhitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双通道神经网络（DualNN）框架，通过参数共享机制高效处理复数信号的实部与虚部，并基于Transformer实现了Dualformer架构，在自动调制识别、信号方案识别和信号结构解析等盲信号分析任务上取得了优于多种基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对盲信号分析任务（如AMR、SSR、SSP）中特征提取器设计的关键挑战，现有实值或复值模型存在泛化误差大或表达能力受限的问题，亟需一种既能降低泛化误差又能保持表达能力的有效框架。

Method: 提出DualNN框架，在IQ通道间共享网络参数以处理复数信号的实部和虚部；基于Transformer实现Dualformer架构，将输入信号分割为patch级token并捕获多粒度特征。

Result: 在AMR、SSR、SSP三项任务上，Dualformer一致优于三个Transformer基线和四个传统DL方法；同时DualNN的模块化设计在盲源分离和低SNR频谱感知等任务上也表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DualNN框架在理论上可降低泛化误差并保持表达能力，在实际任务中实现了鲁棒的跨任务性能提升，为无监督和弱监督复数信号分析场景提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Designing effective feature extractors is critical for blind signal analysis tasks such as automatic modulation recognition (AMR), signal scheme recognition (SSR), and \color{black} signal structure parsing (SSP). In this work, we propose dual-channel neural network (DualNN) that efficiently exploits complex-valued signals through parameter sharing across IQ channels. Unlike traditional real-valued or complex-valued models, DualNN is a groundbreaking framework which shares the network parameters for processing the real and imaginary parts of the complex-valued signals, and is theoretically shown to reduce generalization error while preserving expressive capacity. Specifically, we propose a novel Transformer-based architecture to implement DualNN, called Dualformer. The Dualformer segments input signals into patch-level tokens and captures multi-granularity features, enabling robust performance across diverse signal analysis tasks. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments comparing Dualformer with three Transformer-based baselines and four conventional DL-based approaches. Results demonstrate consistent performance improvements on AMR, SSR, and SSP tasks. Besides, the modular design of DualNN allows it to generalize well to blind signal processing tasks such as blind source separation and low-SNR spectrum sensing. This work paves the way for a broader application of DualNN architectures in unsupervised and weakly supervised complex-valued signal analysis scenarios.

</details>


### [120] [Calibrating the Evaluator: Does Probability Calibration Mitigate Preference Coupling in LLM Agent Feedback Loops?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31371)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了通过概率校准技术来减轻大语言模型智能体在评估反馈中的偏好耦合问题，实验表明校准可将耦合系数降低20-49%，JS散度降低45-67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已发现评估者偏好耦合现象并建立了诊断框架（EPC），但未研究校准技术能否缓解该效应。

Method: 在受试者内实验（N=5）中，比较标准二元TTRL（赢/输）与置信度校准TTRL（概率加权更新），使用DeepSeek-V4-Pro作为执行器、GLM5.2作为评估器，并通过对称LR对照实验排除更新不对称性的影响。

Result: 校准将耦合系数γ降低20-49%，Jensen-Shannon散度降低45-67%。

Conclusion: 概率校准是一种轻量级缓解LLM-as-judge部署中偏好耦合的有效方法，发布了校准TTRL协议并推荐其作为部署流水线中的缓解措施。

Abstract: When large language model (LLM) agents adapt their behavior through evaluator feedback, systematic evaluator biases propagate into the agent's learned strategy distribution - a phenomenon termed evaluator preference coupling. Prior work has documented this coupling and established a diagnostic framework (EPC) to measure it, but has not investigated whether calibration techniques can mitigate the effect. We present the first study of evaluator calibration as mitigation: applying probability calibration to the evaluator's pairwise judgments to reduce spurious preference propagation. In a controlled within-subjects experiment (N=5) comparing standard binary TTRL (win/loss) with confidence-calibrated TTRL (probability-weighted updates) using DeepSeek-V4-Pro as executor and GLM5.2 as evaluator, we find that calibration reduces the coupling coefficient gamma by 20-49% and Jensen-Shannon divergence by 45-67%. A symmetric-LR control confirms the effect is not due to reduced update asymmetry. We release the calibrated TTRL protocol and recommend it as a lightweight mitigation for LLM-as-judge deployment pipelines.

</details>


### [121] [Resolving superposition in AI for interpretability and cross-modal alignment in patient-neuronal images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31394)
*Jisung Park,Seohyeon Kang,Daeun Yoo,Eunsu Lee,Seoin Cho,Wooyeop Choi,Ian Choi,James R. Evan,Daesoo Kim,Sonia Gandhi,Minee L. Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）解决高维生物数据中的叠加（superposition）问题，恢复潜空间几何保真度，并通过Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输将图像表示与单细胞RNA-seq数据对齐，构建无参考空间生物学的可扩展框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物数据中存在维度瓶颈，神经网络迫使不同概念在低维空间中叠加，严重污染潜空间几何结构且被前人忽视。作者旨在解决叠加对表征度量空间的破坏，并实现跨模态生物数据对齐。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在超过10万张帕金森病与健康神经元的多重成像数据上训练以消除叠加；将几何纯净的表征视为单细胞状态向量，将scRNA-seq分析方法直接迁移至图像域；提出GW-map，利用Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输实现图像表征与scRNA-seq数据的从头对齐。

Result: 理论和实验证明叠加污染了表征度量空间，SAE成功恢复几何保真度；GW-map无需参考空间转录组数据即可重建层次化的神经病理通路（如Calcium-AIS scaffold）。

Conclusion: 该方法建立了无参考空间生物学的可扩展基础，为高维生物数据的可解释性和跨模态整合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming our capability to solve biological challenges. In dimensionality bottleneck regimes exacerbated by high-dimensional biological data, Neural networks force distinct concepts into the lower dimensions known as superposition. Although this superposition is widely known to hinder interpretability, its impact on corrupting the geometry of latent spaces remains critically overlooked. Here, we utilized sparse autoencoders (SAEs) trained on over 100,000 multiplexed images of patient-derived Parkinson's disease and healthy neurons to resolve superposition. This approach bypasses the mathematical non-uniqueness of feature attribution by shifting to interpretable latent representation analysis. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that superposition contaminates representational metric spaces, and thereby SAEs successfully recover geometric fidelity. By treating these geometrically purified representations as single-cell state vectors, we adapted single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) data analysis methodologies directly to the image domain. Finally, we introduce GW-map, utilizing Gromov-Wasserstein optimal transport to align these image representations with authentic scRNA-seq data \emph{de novo}. This coupling reconstructs hierarchical neuronal pathology pathways such as Calcium-AIS scaffold, without reference spatial transcriptomics, establishing a scalable foundation for spatial biology. Code is available at https://github.com/jijihihi/Bio_superposition

</details>


### [122] [Mixture-of-Control: State-Aware Fine-Tuning for Transformer-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31397)
*Duc Anh Nguyen,Tien Ngoc Luu,Tung Pham,Toan Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mixture-of-Control (MoC)框架，通过稀疏混合专家机制实现跨Transformer块的局部与全局控制信号自适应融合，在保持状态微调方法内存与计算效率的同时提升表示学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于状态的微调方法仅进行逐块控制更新，限制了跨块信息交换和表示适应能力；而先前实现跨块通信的机制又引入了大量计算开销，降低了高效微调的实用性。

Method: 提出Mixture-of-Control (MoC)轻量级微调框架，将块级控制状态视为稀疏混合专家过程中的专家，实现跨Transformer块的高效通信，自适应整合局部与全局控制信号。

Result: 在多种基于Transformer的基准测试中，MoC优于现有基于状态的微调方法，同时保持了相当的内存和计算效率。

Conclusion: MoC通过稀疏混合专家机制有效解决了现有状态微调方法跨块信息交换受限的问题，在保持高效性的前提下提升了表示适应能力。

Abstract: State-based fine-tuning has emerged as a compelling alternative to weight-based adaptation for transformers, updating lightweight controls into states rather than model weights, offering substantial memory savings while retaining parameter efficiency. However, most existing state-based methods typically apply only per-block control updates, which limits inter-block information exchange and restricts representational adaptation. Meanwhile, prior mechanisms that enable cross-block communication often introduce considerable computational overhead, reducing their practicality for efficient fine-tuning. We introduce Mixture-of-Control (MoC), a lightweight fine-tuning framework that adaptively integrates local and global control signals to enhance representation learning. MoC treats block-wise control states as experts in a sparse mixture-of-experts process, enabling efficient communication across transformer blocks. Empirical results across diverse transformer-based benchmarks demonstrate that MoC outperforms state-based methods while maintaining a comparable memory and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [123] [Contextual Slate GLM Bandits with Limited Adaptivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31449)
*Tanmay Goyal,Sukruta Prakash Midigeshi,Gaurav Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限自适应下的广义线性奖励上下文板条强盗问题，提出了两种算法：B-SlateGLinCB（批处理设置）和RS-SlateGLinCB（极少切换设置），分别在$O(\log\log T)$批次和$O(Nd\log T)$次参数更新下实现了$O(Nd^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$和$O(Nd\sqrt{T})$的遗憾界，且界值不依赖于GLM的非线性参数$\kappa$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中（如推荐系统）的板条选择问题常面临奖励非线性、组合空间巨大以及通信/计算资源受限等挑战，现有方法要么需要完全自适应（频繁更新），要么无法处理广义线性奖励。本文旨在设计在有限自适应条件下仍能高效、理论有保障的板条强盗算法。

Method: 针对批处理设置，提出B-SlateGLinCB，将时间地平线划分为$O(\log\log T)$个批次，每批策略仅依赖前批数据；针对极少切换设置，提出RS-SlateGLinCB，仅执行$O(Nd\log T)$次参数更新。两种算法均利用广义线性模型建模奖励，并通过多样性假设推导遗憾界。

Result: B-SlateGLinCB和RS-SlateGLinCB分别达到$O(Nd^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$和$O(Nd\sqrt{T})$的遗憾界，且不依赖于非线性参数$\kappa$。算法计算高效，每轮仅需$\text{poly}(N)$时间（尽管板条数为$2^{\Omega(N)}$）。仿真实验表明，有限自适应算法优于现有基线，且与最先进的完全自适应算法Slate-GLM-OFU性能相当。

Conclusion: 本文成功在有限自适应约束下解决了广义线性奖励的板条强盗问题，算法在理论和实证上均表现优异，并在语言模型上下文示例选择等实际任务中展现出强性能。

Abstract: We investigate the contextual slate bandit problem with generalized linear rewards under limited adaptivity. At each round, the learner is presented with $N$ sets of items, where each item is represented by a $d$-dimensional feature vector. The learner then constructs a slate by selecting one item per set; the resulting slate yields a scalar reward sampled from a Generalized Linear Model (GLM). We propose algorithms under two limited-adaptivity settings: (a) Batched and (b) Rarely-Switching. For the batched setting, we introduce B-SlateGLinCB, which partitions the time horizon into $\mathcal{O}(\log\log T)$ batches such that each batch's policy relies only on data from previous batches. For the rarely-switching setting, we propose RS-SlateGLinCB, which adaptively performs only $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log T)$ parameter updates. Under a diversity assumption on the item sequences, we prove that B-SlateGLinCB and RS-SlateGLinCB achieve regret bounds of $\mathcal{O}(Nd^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$ and $\mathcal{O}(Nd\sqrt{T})$, respectively. Notably, both bounds are independent of the non-linearity parameter $κ$ that is typically found to scale the regret of GLM bandit algorithms. Our algorithms are computationally efficient, requiring only $\text{poly}(N)$ time per round despite $2^{Ω(N)}$ possible slates. Simulations show our algorithms outperform existing baselines with limited adaptivity and remain competitive with Slate-GLM-OFU, a fully adaptive state-of-the-art algorithm. Notably, a slightly modified B-SlateGLinCB empirically matches this baseline. Finally, we demonstrate strong performance in a practical in-context example selection task for language models.

</details>


### [124] [Zero-Shot Quantization for Object Detectors using Off-the-Shelf Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31456)
*Hyunho Lee,Kyomin Hwang,Hyeonjin Kim,Suyoung Kim,Sunghyun Wee,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GoodQ提出了一种利用现成生成模型构建训练集的零样本量化感知训练（QAT）流水线，通过信息密集提示、内在分布感知选择和教师引导自适应噪声降低三大策略，在低比特（W4A4、W3A3）目标检测零样本量化任务上实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本量化感知训练（QAT）方法在目标检测任务中通过噪声优化合成训练集，但在低比特区域难以维持性能，因此需要更有效的训练集构建方法。

Method: 1) 信息密集提示策略（Information-Dense Prompting）：生成包含多个实例的密集图像；2) 内在分布感知选择（Intrinsic Distribution-Aware Selection）：匹配预训练数据集的类别分布；3) 教师引导自适应噪声降低（Teacher-guided Adaptive Noise Reduction）：缓解QAT过程中伪标签的噪声信号。

Result: 在低比特零样本量化（W4A4）上达到最先进性能，并将量化推广至极低比特宽度（W3A3）。

Conclusion: GoodQ通过引入现成生成模型并针对性地解决三个核心挑战，有效提升了零样本目标检测量化的性能，尤其在极低比特条件下表现优异。

Abstract: With an increasing number of Object Detection (OD) models being deployed on edge devices, Zero-Shot Quantization for OD (ZSQ-OD) aims to quantize these models when access to the original training data is prohibited. Existing research on Zero-Shot Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) for OD synthesizes training sets through noise optimization. However, this approach struggles to maintain performance in low-bit regions. In this paper, we introduce GoodQ (Generative off-the-shelf models for object detector Quantization), a QAT pipeline that utilizes off-the-shelf generative models to construct a training set. We first identify three challenges that arise when introducing a generative model to the ZSQ-OD task: 1) each image contains dense information with multiple instances, 2) the class-wise distribution in the original dataset is imbalanced, and 3) the pseudo-labels assigned to the generated images can potentially act as noisy signals during QAT. GoodQ addresses these challenges by 1) introducing an Information-Dense Prompting strategy to generate multi-instance images, 2) applying Intrinsic Distribution-Aware Selection to match the pretrained class distribution, and 3) employing Teacher-guided Adaptive Noise Reduction to mitigate noise arising from the QAT process. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance in low-bit ZSQ (W4A4) and extends quantization to extreme bit-widths (W3A3). Furthermore, we conduct an extensive analysis to uncover the underlying factors contributing to the efficacy of GoodQ.

</details>


### [125] [TabPATE: Differentially Private Tabular In-Context Learning Without Public Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31474)
*Dariush Wahdany,Matthew Jagielski,Jesse C. Cresswell,Adam Dziedzic,Franziska Boenisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabPATE，一种针对表格基础模型上下文学习中成员推理攻击的差分隐私防御方法，无需公开同分布数据即可保护私有上下文中的记录隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在上下文学习中使用私有数据时，即使基本的成员推理攻击也能成功泄露上下文中的记录隐私，亟需形式化的隐私保护机制。

Method: TabPATE采用PATE框架，将私有上下文划分给多个教师模型，在合成表格查询上私密聚合标签，发布带标签的查询作为学生上下文。表格特征有界且低维，可直接从特征范围或轻度私有化边际分布生成有用查询。

Result: 在多个表格基准测试中，TabPATE在保持有竞争力的效用的同时，将成员推理攻击成功率降至接近随机水平。

Conclusion: TabPATE提供了一条无需公开数据即可实现私有表格上下文学习的实用路径。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models enable accurate in-context learning (ICL) from small labeled datasets, but the private records placed in context can leak through model predictions. We first show that even basic membership inference attacks succeed against tabular ICL, motivating formal privacy protection. We then introduce TabPATE, a differentially private PATE-style defense for tabular ICL that does not require public in-distribution data. TabPATE partitions the private context across teacher models, privately aggregates their labels on synthetic tabular queries, and releases the resulting labeled queries as a student context. Because tabular features are bounded and relatively low-dimensional, useful queries can be generated from feature ranges alone or from lightly privatized marginals. Across tabular benchmarks, TabPATE preserves competitive utility while reducing membership inference to near-random success, providing a practical path to private tabular ICL without public data.

</details>


### [126] [Fork-Think with Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31484)
*Zena Al-Khalili,Rafi Hakim,Dietrich Klakow,Ji-Ung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fork-think方法，采用"先决策后思考"范式，通过识别单条推理路径中的分叉点进行并行采样与聚合，在保持或提升推理性能的同时，将token消耗降低30%、运行时间降低57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行推理方法遵循"先思考后决策"范式，即先采样多条推理路径再进行剪枝或停止，这导致不必要的过度生成。本文探索"先决策后思考"这一未被充分研究的范式，旨在避免过度生成的同时保持推理性能。

Method: Fork-think方法首先在单条种子路径上利用模型置信度识别分叉点，然后在这些分叉点触发并行思考，采样多个延续路径并进行聚合以生成最终响应。

Result: 在三个模型和三个推理基准上的实验表明，Fork-think将token消耗降低最多30%，运行时间降低最多57%，同时性能与并行推理相当或更优。分析发现该方法能识别有意义的任务相关分叉点，且在后置位置采样能产生更好的生成结果。与早期停止和加权投票等机制结合可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 预定分叉策略是高效LLM推理的一个有前景的研究方向，无需预热或离线训练即可实现与现有最先进方法相当的性能。

Abstract: Parallel thinking has enjoyed great success for boosting LLM performance on reasoning tasks without the need for any re-training. However, existing methods follow a think-first-then-decide paradigm, i.e., they first sample multiple reasoning paths, which inevitably leads to overgeneration, then prune or stop unnecessary paths to compensate. In contrast, decide-first-then-think, i.e., first identifying points that are likely to lead to desirable generations, has been underexplored so far. Following this paradigm, we propose Fork-think with confidence, that first identifies forking points using model confidence in a single seeding path, then triggers thinking, sampling multiple continuations and aggregating them for the final response. Our experiments across three models and three reasoning benchmarks show that Fork-think reduces the token consumption by up to 30% and run-time by up to 57%, while performing comparable to or better than parallel thinking. Our analysis reveals that Fork-think is able to identify forking points that are meaningful with respect to the downstream task and that sampling at later positions can lead to substantially better generations. Finally, we demonstrate how combining Fork-think with existing mechanisms such as early stopping and weighted voting can further boost the performance and perform comparably to existing state-of-the-art methods, without requiring any warm-up or offline training. Our results establish pre-determined forking as a promising research direction for efficient LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [127] [RaBitQCache: Rotated Binary Quantization for KVCache in Long Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31519)
*Wenhao Li,Jinhao Dong,Hailin Zhang,Wenhang Shi,Wei Lu,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RaBitQCache提出了一种基于随机旋转二元量化和二元-INT4算术的新型稀疏注意力框架，通过无偏估计器实现自适应Top-p检索，动态调整token预算，并采用异步流水线和延迟更新等硬件感知设计，显著加速长上下文LLM推理并减少内存I/O，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理受限于巨大的KV缓存，现有稀疏注意力方法要么采用静态固定预算（Top-k）检索，要么依赖计算昂贵且有偏的代理分数，亟需一种高效且无偏的稀疏注意力方案。

Method: 利用随机旋转二元量化（RaBitQ）和二元-INT4算术高效估计注意力权重；设计无偏估计器作为代理分数并具有理论误差界，实现自适应Top-p检索动态调整token预算；采用异步流水线和延迟更新等硬件感知系统设计来掩盖开销。

Result: 相比现有最先进的基线方法，RaBitQCache显著加速了推理速度并减少了内存I/O，同时保持了生成质量。

Conclusion: RaBitQCache通过无偏估计和自适应检索机制，在长上下文场景下有效平衡了推理效率与生成质量，为KV缓存稀疏化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context Large Language Model inference is severely bottlenecked by the massive Key-Value (KV) cache, yet existing sparse attention methods often suffer from static fixed-budget (Top-k) retrieval or rely on proxy scores that are computationally expensive and biased. To address these limitations, we propose RaBitQCache, a novel sparse attention framework that utilizes randomized rotated binary quantization and high-throughput binary-INT4 arithmetic to efficiently estimate attention weights. Our proxy score serves as an unbiased estimator with a proven error bound, enabling adaptive Top-p retrieval that dynamically adjusts the token budget based on actual attention sparsity. We further implement a hardware-aware system with asynchronous pipelining and lazy updates to mask overhead. Evaluations demonstrate that RaBitQCache significantly accelerates inference and reduces memory I/O while preserving generation quality compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/Sakuraaa0/RaBitQCache.git.

</details>


### [128] [On the Convergence of Self-Improving Online LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31524)
*Xudong Wu,Pangpang Liu,Vaneet Aggarwal,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对SAIL算法在分布偏移问题上的收敛性分析缺失，发现其标准目标函数因Hessian性质不佳而非强凹，提出引入反向KL散度正则化的SAIL-RevKL目标，证明了其在有界参数空间内满足PL条件，建立了全局收敛保证和近线性样本复杂度，在MuJoCo和LLM对齐任务上优于原始SAIL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAIL算法在分布偏移问题上表现良好，但缺乏形式化的收敛性分析；进一步发现标准SAIL目标函数因Hessian性质不利而不保证强凹性，需要理论上的改进与保证。

Method: 提出SAIL-RevKL，在目标函数中加入反向KL散度惩罚项以改善优化景观；通过理论分析证明该正则化目标在有界参数空间内满足Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL)条件，从而建立全局收敛保证和近线性样本复杂度分析。

Result: 理论方面：证明了SAIL-RevKL满足PL条件，建立了全局收敛保证，达到近线性样本复杂度。实验方面：在MuJoCo基准和LLM对齐任务上，SAIL-RevKL在效果和稳定性上均优于原始SAIL。

Conclusion: 反向KL散度正则化有效改善了SAIL的优化景观，使目标函数满足PL条件，从而获得理论上的收敛保证，同时实际性能也得到提升。

Abstract: The Self-Improving Alignment (SAIL) algorithm addresses distribution shift by reducing a bilevel formulation of the problem to an efficient, single-level method. Empirically, SAIL has demonstrated strong performance on this task. However, a formal analysis of its convergence properties has been lacking. We identify a key theoretical challenge: the standard SAIL objective function is not guaranteed to be strongly concave due to unfavorable properties of its Hessian. To address this limitation, we propose a regularized objective, SAIL-RevKL, which incorporates a reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence penalty to improve the optimization landscape. Our central theoretical contribution is to prove that this regularized objective satisfies the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition within a bounded parameter space. We establish global convergence guarantees, achieving a near-linear sample complexity. We further validate the effectiveness and stability of SAIL-RevKL through empirical evaluations, demonstrating that it outperforms the vanilla SAIL on both MuJoCo benchmarks and LLM alignment tasks.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond the Expressivity-Trainability Paradox: A Dynamical Lie Algebra Perspective on Navigating Barren Plateaus in Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31536)
*Kung-Ming Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量子机器学习中一个反直觉的现象：无结构的量子架构并非过拟合，而是处于严重的欠拟合状态。作者通过动态李代数（DLA）和几何QML框架，证明参数化量子电路（PQC）的巨大希尔伯特空间容量恰是导致贫瘠高原（BP）的数学根源，并提出通过群论几何先验作为结构正则化器，在保持可训练性的前提下实现多项式级DLA增长，为可扩展量子神经网络提供了"可训练性-by-Design"路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前QML从理论走向实践面临关键架构瓶颈，经典统计学习理论的基本假设受到挑战。经典深度学习中增加模型容量通常会带来过拟合风险，但无结构的量子架构却呈现出量子欠拟合这一反直觉现象，其根源在于"表达性-可训练性悖论"。

Method: 综合动态李代数（DLA）和几何QML的最新突破，建立了电路生成器的代数维度与优化动力学之间的数学框架；并在非线性二分类任务上进行实证验证，对比了无结构架构（量子过拟合）与引入群论几何先验的结构化方法（作为结构正则化器）。

Result: 无结构架构通过不可扩展的参数化实现近乎完美的训练准确率（量子过拟合），而嵌入几何先验的方法通过将DLA增长限制在多项式范围内，牺牲原始记忆容量以换取可扩展的、梯度丰富的训练景观。

Conclusion: 量子机器学习的关键并非追求更大的希尔伯特空间容量，而在于通过几何先验进行结构化设计，实现"可训练性-by-Design"，从而构建可扩展的量子神经网络。

Abstract: As Quantum Machine Learning (QML) transitions toward practical implementation, the field faces a critical architectural bottleneck that challenges the fundamental assumptions of classical statistical learning theory. In classical deep learning, increasing model capacity typically risks overfitting. However, this study advances a counter-intuitive paradigm: unstructured contemporary QML architectures suffer from a profound state of quantum underfitting, driven by the "expressivity-trainability paradox." We demonstrate that the vast Hilbert space capacity of Parameterized Quantum Circuits (PQCs)-traditionally chased as the source of quantum advantage is the direct mathematical cause of Barren Plateaus (BPs), where gradient landscapes become exponentially flat. By synthesizing recent breakthroughs in Dynamical Lie Algebras (DLAs) and Geometric QML, we establish a comprehensive framework linking the algebraic dimension of circuit generators to their optimization dynamics. Furthermore, we empirically validate this framework on a non-linear binary classification task, illuminating a uniquely quantum manifestation of the bias-variance tradeoff: while unstructured architectures achieve near-perfect training accuracy via unscalable parameterization (quantum overfitting), embedding group-theoretic geometric priors acts as a structural regularizer. By restricting the DLA growth to a polynomial regime, our symmetry-preserving approach sacrifices raw memorization capacity to guarantee scalable, gradient-rich training landscapes, offering a robust roadmap for "Trainability-by-Design" in scalable quantum neural networks.

</details>


### [130] [Introduction to Stochastic Differential Equations for Generative Machine Learning: A Variational Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31576)
*Ole Winther,Paul Jeha,Sander Dieleman,Andriy Mnih,Manfred Opper,Andrea Dittadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提供了关于常微分方程和随机微分方程在生成式机器学习中应用的自我包含的非正式介绍，涵盖了扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配的变分下界推导及统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为生成式机器学习中基于微分方程的方法（扩散模型、分数匹配、流匹配）提供一个统一且易于理解的变分框架介绍，帮助读者理解这些方法之间的内在联系。

Method: 通过推导变分下界（ELBO）作为通用出发点，将扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配视为该变分框架的不同参数化方式；利用Fokker-Planck方程描述随机变量的边缘分布随时间演化；并通过一维密度建模问题对不同参数化进行比较。

Result: 成功建立了一个统一视角，将扩散模型、分数匹配和流匹配解释为最一般变分方法的具体参数化形式。

Conclusion: 基于微分方程的生成模型（扩散、分数匹配、流匹配）可以通过变分下界统一理解，不同方法本质上是对同一变分框架的不同参数化选择。

Abstract: The use of ordinary and stochastic differential equations has led to substantial progress in generative machine learning with applications to, for example, image, video and biomolecule generation. This paper provides a self-contained and informal introduction to the differential equations, the probabilistic framework for using them in generative modeling and the Fokker--Planck equation that governs the temporal evolution of the marginal distribution of the stochastic variables of the differential equations. The variational lower bound on the log-likelihood (the evidence lower bound, ELBO) is derived and used as a general starting point for a discussion of diffusion models, score matching, and flow matching. All of these approaches may be viewed as specific parameterizations of the most general variational approach. A one-dimensional density modeling problem is used as a simple example to compare different parameterizations.

</details>


### [131] [Robustness of neural networks to random noise perturbations of their inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31581)
*Mark Levene,Martyn Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络的输入扰动鲁棒性问题，提出了一种高效的黑盒鲁棒性度量方法，能够在高概率下给出网络在输入扰动下的均方误差上界，并通过实验和鲁棒性曲线验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经网络的实际应用中，输入数据往往存在噪声或扰动，但现有研究较少关注网络精度（均方误差）与鲁棒性之间的内在关系，缺乏一种简单高效且能提供理论保证的鲁棒性度量方法。

Method: 提出一种将神经网络视为黑盒的鲁棒性度量方法，该度量以高概率给出在输入扰动下网络均方误差的上界；同时引入鲁棒性曲线的概念，用于分析数据集内部及跨数据集的鲁棒性特征。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够有效评估神经网络的鲁棒性，鲁棒性曲线为深入分析鲁棒性提供了新的视角。

Conclusion: 本文的鲁棒性度量方法兼顾简洁性与计算效率，无需了解网络内部结构即可使用，为神经网络在噪声环境下的可靠性评估提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of the robustness of a trained neural network to the perturbation of its input values. More specifically, we examine the interplay between the accuracy of the network, as measured by the mean squared error, and robustness. Accordingly, we present a robustness measure, which, with high probability, suggests an upper bound on the mean squared error of the network, with respect to an input data set, for a given perturbation of the input values of the network. The measure we propose is both simple and efficient to compute, treating the neural network as a black box. We provide experimental results on several real-world data sets showing the efficacy of the proposed method. We also introduce the concept of robustness curves, which allows us to further analyse robustness within and between data sets.

</details>


### [132] [Evil Spectra: How Optimisers can Amplify or Suppress Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31591)
*Jason R. Brown,Patrick Leask,Lev McKinney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型中"突发性错位"（Emergent Misalignment, EM）现象对优化器选择的敏感性，发现优化器选择是EM严重程度的最大影响因素（导致7倍错位率差异），而模型规模影响可忽略；通过谱正则化可以显著缓解EM易发优化器（如Adam和Lion）的错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究注意到EM的严重程度高度敏感于训练选择，但缺乏对这一敏感性的系统性刻画。

Method: 对多个Qwen3模型、优化器、数据集和批大小进行扫描实验；在12个不同规模（1B-235B）和家族的模型上使用Adam进行额外扫描；分析Qwen3-8B上的损失-对齐关系；引入谱正则化损失项激励更平坦的奇异值谱。

Result: 优化器选择产生7倍错位率差异；模型规模在Qwen3家族内影响可忽略；最终对数训练损失是对齐的强预测因子；优化器在训练后期比对训练损失更重要的对齐预测因子；Muon优化器通过隐式正则化使LoRA适配器奇异值分布更均匀来保持对齐；谱正则化能在训练损失几乎不变的情况下有效恢复对齐。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是EM严重程度的关键因素，但谱正则化可以显著缓解EM易发优化器的影响。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is a recently discovered phenomenon in LLMs where fine-tuning on a narrow misaligned task, such as writing insecure code, leads to broadly misaligned behaviour on unrelated prompts. Previous work has noted that the severity of EM is highly sensitive to training choices; however, we still lack a systematic characterisation of this sensitivity. We perform a sweep over several Qwen3 models, optimisers, datasets, and batch sizes, and find that the choice of optimiser has the largest effect, producing a 7x spread in misalignment rate. Surprisingly, model size has a negligible effect within the Qwen3 family. An additional sweep over 12 models from three families using Adam confirms that model scale (1B-235B) and family have negligible effects for that optimiser. Analysing the loss-alignment relationship on Qwen3-8B, we find that final log training loss is a strong predictor of alignment, and that stratifying by optimiser captures nearly all the residual variance. Training dynamics reveal that each optimiser follows a different trajectory through loss-alignment space, and that after significant training, the optimiser becomes more important than training loss as a predictor of alignment. Muon, the adaptive optimiser that preserves alignment the best, implicitly regularises for a more uniform distribution of singular values of the LoRA adapter. We evaluate this insight by training with an additional loss term that incentivises a flatter singular value spectrum, and find that this substantially recovers alignment for the more EM-prone adaptive optimisers (Adam and Lion), with negligible cost to training loss. These results identify optimiser choice as a key factor in EM severity, but show that spectral regularisation can substantially mitigate the effects of EM-prone optimisers.

</details>


### [133] [Calibration, Not Compilation: Detecting and Repairing Misspecified Probabilistic Programs Written by Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31630)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将贝叶斯工作流（后验预测检查、模拟校准、采样器诊断等）作为概率程序的正确性验证器，而非传统单元测试。实验表明：在检测上，校准方法对14种错误类型AUC达0.97（参考程序已知）和62-78%（无参考程序），远超单元测试（0%）；在修复上，校准反馈显著优于单元测试反馈（后者甚至比无反馈更差）；在实际场景中，LLM编写的可运行程序存在15-47%的统计错误，校准引导修复优于LLM自评和贝叶斯检查清单等方法。结论：对于概率程序，正确性=校准，而非编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单元测试无法检测概率程序中的统计错误（如错误似然、无效先验、病态参数化等），需要更合适的验证工具来确保统计正确性。

Method: 将贝叶斯工作流（后验预测检查、模拟校准、采样器诊断如Rhat、散度、ESS，以及留出预测密度）作为校准验证器，从检测（有/无参考程序）、修复（LLM循环中对比反馈类型）、现实评估三个维度进行系统研究。

Result: 检测：有参考程序时AUC 0.97（2% FPR下88%召回），无参考程序时62-78%，单元测试0%。修复：校准反馈显著优于单元测试反馈，后者甚至比无反馈更差。现实：LLM生成的可运行程序中15-47%存在统计错误，校准引导修复效果最佳。

Conclusion: 概率程序的正确性应通过校准而非编译来验证；单元测试在统计错误检测中完全失效，甚至可能因虚假信心阻碍修复；校准反馈是更有效的验证与修复工具。

Abstract: Language models increasingly write probabilistic programs (in NumPyro, Stan, or Pyro), but a program that compiles, runs, and passes every unit test can still be \emph{statistically} wrong -- a Gaussian likelihood for heavy-tailed data, a Poisson for over-dispersed counts, an invalid prior support, or a pathological parameterization. The right verifier is therefore not a test suite but the Bayesian workflow itself: posterior predictive checks, simulation-based calibration, sampler diagnostics ($\hat R$, divergences, ESS), and held-out predictive density. We study this calibration oracle along three axes. \textbf{Detection:} on a benchmark of $14$ misspecification types across $10$ model families ($200$ instances), it flags the bug with AUC $0.97$ ($88\%$ at $2\%$ FPR \emph{when handed the correct reference program, an upper bound}) -- and a fully \emph{reference-free} version that uses no correct program reaches $62$--$78\%$ (the upper figure from a small automated model search), versus $0\%$ for a unit-test oracle. \textbf{Repair:} used as feedback in an LLM repair loop across fifteen models, calibration significantly outperforms unit-test feedback -- which is itself \emph{significantly worse than no feedback at all}, a passing test inducing false confidence that suppresses repair -- and improves over no feedback on strong-but-unsaturated models (GPT-5.1 $33{\to}92\%$, Claude $75{\to}100\%$; paired McNemar, $n{=}228$). \textbf{Reality:} on programs LLMs write from scratch for neutral briefs, $15$--$47\%$ of runnable ones are statistically misspecified (unit tests catch none), and calibration-guided repair significantly beats LLM-as-judge review, a Bayesian-workflow checklist, and data-summary self-debug. Across all three, the lesson is the same: for probabilistic programs, correctness is calibration, not compilation.

</details>


### [134] [ECHO: Prune to act, trace to learn with selective turn memory in agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31650)
*Zijun Xie,Binbin Zheng,Enlei Gong,Jihua Liu,Yuyang You,Lingfeng Liu,Jiayao Tang,Guanqun Zhao,Aoqi Hu,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ECHO提出了一种选择性回合记忆框架，通过源索引重建来解决长程语言智能体在有限上下文窗口下的历史坍缩和可追溯学习问题，在BrowseComp-Plus上达到43.4%的准确率，显著优于GRPO (28.9%)和SUPO (36.1%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文管理方法在处理长程交互时存在两个耦合局限：一是随着轮次增加，历史观测被逐步移除或压缩，使得策略难以复用细粒度证据；二是原始轮次失去源可寻址性后，基于结果的强化学习无法将策略更新与支持正确答案的证据进行显式对齐。

Method: ECHO框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将每个完整的环境交互轮次压缩为紧凑的记忆记录；2) 通过从这些记录中选择来重建有界策略上下文；3) 利用选定的源索引将正向结果信用路由到支持正确答案的证据和选择动作。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus上，ECHO达到43.4%的留出准确率，优于GRPO (28.9%)和SUPO (36.1%)，且使用的轮次和轨迹量更少。训练后的策略在稠密和MoE骨干网络上均展现出跨多目标QA、代码生成和深度信息搜索基准的零样本泛化提升。

Conclusion: ECHO通过源索引重建同时解决了历史坍缩和可追溯学习问题，为长程语言智能体提供了更高效的上下文管理和强化学习对齐方法，并在多个基准上验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents must repeatedly interact with tools, accumulate evidence, and make decisions under bounded context windows. Existing context-management methods make such rollouts feasible by truncating distant history, folding past turns into summaries, or selecting compact memory states. However, these breakthroughs introduce two coupled limitations. First, as the number of turns grows, historical observations are progressively removed or collapsed into compressed states, making it harder for the policy to reuse fine-grained evidence. Second, once the original turns are no longer source-addressable, outcome-based RL loses an explicit path for aligning policy updates with the evidence that supported a successful final answer. To this end, we propose ECHO, a selective turn-memory framework that jointly addresses history collapse and traceable learning through source-indexed reconstruction. Specifically, ECHO compresses each completed environment turn into a compact memory record, reconstructs bounded policy contexts by selecting from these records, and reuses the selected source indices to route positive outcome credit to the evidence and selection actions that support successful answers. On BrowseComp-Plus, ECHO reaches 43.4% held-out accuracy, outperforming GRPO (28.9%) and the rolling-summary baseline SUPO (36.1%), while using fewer turns and lower trajectory volume than SUPO (Figure 1). Additionally, the trained policy improves zero-shot generalization across multi-objective QA, code generation, and deep information-seeking benchmarks on both dense and MoE backbones.

</details>


### [135] [Improving Certified Robustness via Adversarial Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31653)
*Matteo Melis,Jesus Martinez Del Rincon,Vishal Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AD-CERT是一种结合对抗性蒸馏与区间边界传播（IBP）上界的认证训练方法，通过在logit空间蒸馏经验鲁棒教师模型的对抗信息，作为认证训练的有效下界替代，在多个鲁棒性基准上实现了最先进的认证性能，并比特征空间蒸馏目标在认证准确率上提升高达5.40个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证训练方法存在标准准确率与认证性能之间的权衡：紧致松弛界方法牺牲标准准确率，而对抗训练虽提升经验鲁棒性和标准准确率但难以认证。近期研究表明，结合对抗训练目标与松散IBP上界可改善权衡。本文旨在进一步利用对抗性蒸馏来提升认证训练的性能。

Method: 提出AD-CERT目标，将对抗性蒸馏与IBP上界结合，在logit空间从经验鲁棒教师模型中蒸馏对抗信息，作为认证训练的下界替代。

Result: AD-CERT在多个鲁棒性基准上达到最先进的认证性能；在统一设置下，logit级别的对抗性蒸馏相比鲁棒特征空间蒸馏目标，认证准确率提升高达5.40个百分点。

Conclusion: logit空间的对抗性蒸馏为认证训练提供了有效的下界替代，AD-CERT有效实现了标准准确率与认证性能间的更好权衡。

Abstract: Certified training aims to produce models whose predictions can be formally verified against adversarial perturbations, typically by optimising upper bounds on the worst-case loss over an allowed perturbation set. For neural networks, certified training methods based purely on tight relaxation bounds produce networks that are amenable to certification, but sacrifice standard accuracy. Conversely, adversarial training often yields stronger empirical robustness and standard accuracy, but the resulting models are generally difficult to certify with neural network verifiers. Recently, the literature has shown that better standard-certified accuracy trade-offs can be achieved by combining adversarial training objectives with loose over-approximations based on Interval Bound Propagation (IBP), effectively interpolating between lower and upper bounds of the worst-case loss. Building on this, we introduce AD-CERT, a certified training objective that combines adversarial distillation with an IBP upper bound. We show that distilling adversarial information over the logit space from an empirically robust teacher provides an effective lower bound surrogate for certified training, with AD-CERT achieving state-of-the-art certified performance on several robustness benchmarks. Furthermore, in a unified setup, distilling adversarial information at the logit-level is shown to improve certified accuracy over a robust feature-space distillation objective by up to 5.40 percentage points.

</details>


### [136] [When to Truncate a Feature Ranking: A Residual-Overlap Stopping Rule for Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31686)
*Jesus S. Aguilar-Ruiz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于风险校准的分布框架，用于将监督特征排序转化为类别无关的特征子集，通过Bhattacharyya系数测量边际分离，并利用残差乘积重叠阈值实现自动停止规则，在高维基因组数据中可将数万特征缩减至数十个且保持预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征排序方法在第一阶段（排序）研究充分，但第二阶段（选择子集）通常依赖任意基数、经验阈值或交叉验证，缺乏直接可解释的停止准则。核心问题：给定特征排序后，何时累积的类别分离证据足够充分，可以停止选择特征？

Method: 1) 对每对类别和每个特征，用Bhattacharyya系数衡量类条件分布间的边际分离程度；2) 为每个相关类别对比设定残差乘积重叠阈值，保留排序前缀中最短的子集使所有类别对比的残差重叠低于阈值；3) 推导二元和多类Bayes风险界，提供基于先验和无先验的阈值校准方法。

Result: 在高维基因组数据集上，该方法能将数万特征缩减至几十个，同时保持与全特征基线统计上相当的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该停止规则仅需一维边际重叠估计并在预计算排序上扫描，适用于极高维场景，其中穷举子集搜索不可行且特征排序的可解释截断至关重要。

Abstract: Feature rankings are widely used in supervised feature selection because they are simple, scalable and easy to interpret. Variables are first ranked by a relevance score, and a subset is then obtained by retaining the top-ranked variables. Although the first stage has been extensively studied, the second is often governed by an arbitrary cardinality, an empirical threshold or cross-validation, without a direct interpretation. This raises a basic question: given a feature ranking, when is there enough accumulated class-separation evidence to stop selecting features?
  This paper develops a distributional framework for transforming supervised feature rankings into class-independent subsets through an explicit risk-calibrated stopping rule. For each variable and each pair of classes, marginal separation is measured by the Bhattacharyya coefficient between the corresponding class-conditional distributions. The proposed method selects a single global subset shared by all classes by retaining the shortest prefix of a ranking whose residual product overlap falls below a prescribed threshold for every relevant class contrast. We derive binary and multiclass Bayes-risk bounds for the labelled product marginal problem, and obtain prior-dependent and prior-free calibrations of the residual-overlap threshold from a target all-pairs risk level.
  An empirical comparison on high-dimensional genomic datasets illustrates that the rule can reduce tens of thousands of variables to a few dozen while maintaining predictive performance statistically comparable to the all-features baseline. As the stopping rule only requires one-dimensional marginal overlap estimates and scans a precomputed ranking, it is well suited to very high-dimensional settings where exhaustive subset search is infeasible and interpretable truncation of feature rankings is essential.

</details>


### [137] [Diffusing Blame: Task-Dependent Credit Assignment in Biologically Plausible Dual-Stream Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31700)
*Yutaro Yamada,Luca Grillotti,Rujikorn Charakorn,Sebastian Risi,David Ha,Robert Tjarko Lange*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模误差路由（modulo error routing）扩展误差扩散（ED）算法，在严格遵循Dale原则的双流兴奋/抑制架构中，首次将ED从二分类扩展到多分类和监督学习（MNIST 96.7%, CIFAR-10 61.7%），并整合PPO实现强化学习（连续控制与开放探索任务），性能与无反向传播基线Direct Feedback Alignment相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经回路遵循Dale原则（每个神经元仅兴奋或仅抑制），但人工网络在满足该约束下难以实现高性能学习，超越MNIST的多分类任务尤为困难；同时需要探索该约束下监督学习和强化学习的统一框架。

Method: 提出模误差路由扩展误差扩散算法；在双流兴奋/抑制架构中引入三类领域专用创新：层特异sigmoid宽度、批中心类误差信号、非对称初始化；在强化学习中将ED与PPO集成（ED-PPO）。

Result: MNIST 96.7%, CIFAR-10 61.7%基线；消融实验显示三个创新组件的重要性在MNIST和CIFAR-10间反转；ED-PPO在Google Brax连续控制和Craftax开放探索任务中达到与Direct Feedback Alignment相当的竞争力。

Conclusion: 严格Dale原则下的表征学习是可行的；任务依赖的信用分配瓶颈需多基准评估才能揭示；ED-PPO可作为无反向传播强化学习的可行方案。

Abstract: Biological neural circuits obey Dale's principle: each neuron's synapses are uniformly excitatory or inhibitory. Artificial networks that respect this constraint must coordinate separate excitatory and inhibitory populations, fundamentally changing how credit is assigned during learning. Several biologically plausible learning rules avoid backpropagation's weight transport requirement, but it has been difficult to achieve strong performance under Dale's principle beyond MNIST. Error Diffusion (ED) was originally proposed in a dual-stream excitatory/inhibitory architecture, where learning is driven by routing global error signals to all layers without transporting transposed forward weights or relying on random feedback matrices. Whether such a rule can scale under Dale's principle across both supervised classification and reinforcement learning remains unknown. Here, we introduce modulo error routing to extend Error Diffusion beyond binary classification, and show that a dual-stream excitatory/inhibitory architecture trained with this method achieves 96.7% on MNIST and establishes a 61.7% baseline on CIFAR-10, demonstrating that representation learning is possible even when strictly enforcing Dale's principle. For the classification setting, we introduce three domain-specific innovations: layer-specific sigmoid widths, batch-centered class error signals, and asymmetric initialization, and ablation analysis reveals that their relative importance reverses between MNIST and CIFAR-10, exposing task-dependent credit-assignment bottlenecks invisible to single-benchmark evaluation. In reinforcement learning, we integrate ED with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and evaluate it on continuous-control tasks in Google Brax and on Craftax, an open-ended exploration task. We show that ED-PPO achieves competitive performance relative to Direct Feedback Alignment, a backpropagation-free baseline.

</details>


### [138] [Nonlinearity-Aware LoRA: Structured Gate Adaptation under Low-Rank Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31717)
*Shuai Yuan,Sudong Cai,Bingzhi Chen,Shuyuan Zheng,Chuan Xiao,Makoto Onizuka,Rui Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NA-LoRA，一种基于非线性感知的低秩微调方法，通过导数重要性掩码和激活步长缩放规则，解决门控FFN中低秩更新导致的selection misalignment问题，在语言和视觉-语言微调任务上超越标准LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA忽略了自门控Transformer前馈网络中低秩残差不仅改变投影特征，还会影响非线性选择权重（门控信号），导致selection misalignment问题，现有微调方法未考虑这一非线性特性。

Method: 提出NA-LoRA，包含两个轻量机制：(1) 基于导数的时序重要性掩码（derivative-based temporal-importance mask），用于门控相关的LoRA更新；(2) 激活特定的步长缩放规则（activation-specific step-scaling rule），在有效的粗粒度局部同质性划分可用时调整更新步长。无辅助损失，无推理开销。

Result: 在语言模型微调和视觉-语言迁移基准上，NA-LoRA一致优于标准LoRA，与强PEFT变体相比具有竞争力或更优。

Conclusion: 低秩微调应遵循非线性感知原则：将容量分配给非线性状态仍保持响应的门控通道，并塑造选择的时间演化。NA-LoRA验证了该原则的有效性。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is commonly viewed as an update-space approximation to full fine-tuning, yet this view is incomplete for self-gated Transformer feed-forward networks. In gated FFNs, a low-rank residual can change not only projected features but also the nonlinear selection weights that determine which channels contribute to the output. We formalize this effect as selection misalignment and connect it to the local effective homogeneity of self-gated activations. This motivates a nonlinearity-aware principle for parameter-efficient fine-tuning: low-rank updates should allocate capacity to gate channels whose nonlinear states remain responsive and should shape the temporal evolution of selection. We propose NA-LoRA, a training-only method with two lightweight mechanisms: a derivative-based temporal-importance mask for gate-related LoRA updates and an activation-specific step-scaling rule when a meaningful coarse effective-homogeneity partition is available. NA-LoRA adds no auxiliary loss and incurs no inference-time overhead. Experiments on language-model fine-tuning and vision-language transfer benchmarks show that NA-LoRA consistently improves over vanilla LoRA and is competitive with or better than strong PEFT variants.

</details>


### [139] [FedXDS: Leveraging Model Attribution Methods to counteract Data Heterogeneity in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31742)
*Maximilian Andreas Hoefler,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedXDS，首个将可解释AI（XAI）中的特征归因技术应用于联邦学习的方法，通过选择性数据共享来缓解客户端数据异构导致的性能下降，并引入度量隐私保护敏感信息，实验证明该方法在准确率和收敛速度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI方法在提升模型可解释性方面已取得成功，但其在联邦学习等相邻机器学习领域的潜力尚未被探索。联邦学习面临客户端数据分布统计异构导致的性能退化问题，现有方法缺乏精确识别哪些数据元素应在客户端间选择性共享的手段。

Method: 提出FedXDS框架，利用基于传播的特征归因技术（通过单次反向传播识别任务相关特征）实现精确的选择性数据共享；结合度量隐私技术提供形式化的隐私保证，在保护敏感信息的同时保持数据效用。

Result: 在不同客户端数量和异构性设置下，FedXDS相比现有方法 consistently 实现更高的准确率和更快的收敛速度；理论层面提供了隐私保证，实验层面展示了对成员推理攻击和特征反转攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 首次验证了XAI特征归因技术在联邦学习场景下的有效性，为缓解数据异构性提供了新的方法论，且在隐私保护方面兼具理论保障和实证鲁棒性。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods have demonstrated significant success in recent years at identifying relevant features in input data that drive deep learning model decisions, enhancing interpretability for users. However, the potential of XAI beyond providing model transparency has remained largely unexplored in adjacent machine learning domains. In this paper, we show for the first time how XAI can be utilized in the context of federated learning. Specifically, while federated learning enables collaborative model training without raw data sharing, it suffers from performance degradation when client data distributions exhibit statistical heterogeneity. We introduce FedXDS (Federated Learning via XAI-guided Data Sharing), the first approach to utilize feature attribution techniques to identify precisely which data elements should be selectively shared between clients to mitigate heterogeneity. By employing propagation-based attribution, our method identifies task-relevant features through a single backward pass, enabling selective data sharing that aligns client contributions. To protect sensitive information, we incorporate metric privacy techniques that provide formal privacy guarantees while preserving utility. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach consistently achieves higher accuracy and faster convergence compared to existing methods across varying client numbers and heterogeneity settings. We provide theoretical privacy guarantees and empirically demonstrate robustness against both membership inference and feature inversion attacks. Code is available at https://github.com/MaxH1996/FedXDS.

</details>


### [140] [Addressing Over-Refusal in LLMs with Competing Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31748)
*Taeyoun Kim,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAR方法，通过让语言模型在安全训练中主动探索有害推理（但最终输出安全回答）来解决过度拒绝问题，利用过程奖励进行对抗优化，有效缓解了安全性与拒绝率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全训练方法常导致过度拒绝——对有害提示安全提升，但对无害提示也增加拒绝。即使使用RL训练模型先推理再回答，推理也可能只是预设答案的"橡皮图章"。因此需要从根本上重新思考安全推理的训练方式。

Method: 将问题建模为对抗优化：推理玩家探索产生不安全回答的策略，回答玩家确保最终输出安全。使用单一模型在同一个思维链的不同段中扮演两个角色，并通过过程奖励（process rewards）实现竞争目标的稳定优化。SEAR（Safe Exploration via Adversarial Reasoning）允许模型在推理阶段充分探索有害内容，但最终输出安全回答。

Result: SEAR能够有意地参与有害推理作为探索，同时可靠地折返回安全回答。该方法有效缓解了过度拒绝问题，并能防御直接操纵推理链使其变得有害的攻击。

Conclusion: 不安全推理本身可以作为有用的探索信号。通过鼓励模型充分探索有害推理但产生安全回答，能提高模型区分有害与无害提示的能力，在保证安全的同时对无害提示正常响应，从而解决安全性与拒绝率之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Safety training on language models often induces over-refusal: improved safety on harmful prompts at the cost of increased refusal on harmless ones. Though this trade-off can be mitigated by training models with reinforcement learning (RL) to reason before answering, it does not remove the underlying problem that reasoning can often be a "rubber stamp" for a predetermined response. In this paper, we address the safety-refusal trade-off by rethinking how models are trained to reason about safety. Our key insight is that unsafe reasoning can itself serve as a useful exploratory signal. Rather than preemptively blocking harmful thoughts, we encourage the model to sufficiently explore unsafe reasoning but produce a safe response. The harmful exploration improves the model's ability to distinguish harmful from harmless prompts by resolving ambiguity, allowing it to remain safe while complying only when appropriate. We cast this as an adversarial optimization problem in which a reasoning player explores strategies for producing an unsafe response and an answer player ensures that the final output is safe. We train a single model with dense rewards to play both roles within one chain-of-thought, across different segments. To achieve this, we find that process rewards are crucial for stable optimization of competing objectives. Our resulting model SEAR deliberately engages in harmful reasoning as exploration while reliably flipping back to a safe answer. We demonstrate that this behavior helps mitigate over-refusal and defend against attacks that directly manipulate the reasoning to be harmful.

</details>


### [141] [Bridging the Gap Between Latent and Explicit Reasoning with Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31779)
*Ying Fan,Anej Svete,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOTUS是一种基于循环Transformer的隐式思维链方法，通过并行监督潜在表示，首次在3B参数规模下达到与显式CoT相当的性能，同时将推理延迟降低2.5-6.9倍，且潜在空间具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式CoT方法在参数超过1B时性能明显低于显式CoT，且差距随规模扩大而增大。循环深度Transformer自然适合隐式推理，但尚未被探索能否弥合这一差距。

Method: 采用循环填充Transformer架构，对K个潜在块并行处理R次迭代，在每个潜在位置上对黄金CoT步骤token施加交叉熵损失（类似于显式CoT监督），实现LOTUS模型。

Result: LOTUS在3B参数规模下首次弥合了隐式CoT与显式CoT之间的性能差距，推理阶段延迟降低2.5-6.9倍，且通过将循环后潜在向量投影到LM头可恢复黄金推理步骤及替代有效中间步骤。

Conclusion: 循环骨干网络和对黄金CoT token的并行监督都是实现有效隐式推理的关键组件；LOTUS的潜在空间具有可解释性且与CoT对齐。

Abstract: Language models typically reason via explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), generating intermediate steps token-by-token. Latent CoT offers an alternative: it performs multi-step reasoning in the model's hidden states, replacing decoded tokens with continuous representations for greater efficiency. However, existing latent CoT methods underperform explicit CoT beyond 1B parameters, and the gap widens with scale. Looped, or recurrent-depth, Transformers, which reuse their weights to increase computation depth without adding parameters, are a natural fit for latent reasoning. We therefore ask whether looped Transformers can bridge this gap. We answer affirmatively with a simple recipe: a looped padded Transformer that processes K latent blocks in parallel for R iterations, with a cross-entropy loss on each latent position's gold CoT-step token, similar to explicit CoT supervision. We instantiate it as LOTUS (Looped Transformers with parallel supervision on latents). LOTUS is, to our knowledge, the first latent-CoT method to bridge the gap to explicit CoT at the 3B scale, while cutting thought-phase latency by 2.5x-6.9x from compact math expressions to natural language. Projecting LOTUS's post-loop latents through the base LM head recovers the gold reasoning steps and even surfaces alternative valid intermediate steps, evidence that its latent space is interpretable and CoT-aligned. Ablations confirm that both the looped backbone and the parallel supervision on gold CoT tokens are essential.

</details>


### [142] [Policy Optimization Achieves Data-Dependent Regret Bounds in MDPs with Unknown Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31769)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在线情节式表格马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中转移核未知的问题，提出了一种基于乐观跟随正则化领导者（OFTRL）的新算法，首次在策略优化框架下实现了同时包含一阶、二阶、路径长度界（数据依赖界）以及随机环境下间隙依赖的polylog(T)遗憾界的最优双世界保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作（Dann et al., 2023; Li et al., 2026）表明策略优化在已知转移核下可适应对抗性与随机性损失，并取得一阶、二阶和路径长度界，但转移核未知时能否实现类似的数据依赖界尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出基于乐观跟随正则化领导者的新算法，核心创新在于设计了新的乐观Q函数估计器，并结合数据依赖的转移奖励（transition bonus），通过损失预测误差控制估计器偏差；同时识别出转移依赖的复杂度项以刻画转移核估计的内在代价。

Result: 在转移核未知条件下，算法同时取得一阶、二阶和路径长度界（含转移依赖复杂度项），并在随机环境下实现间隙依赖的polylog(T)遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明策略优化在未知转移核下也能获得数据依赖的遗憾界，揭示了转移核估计引入的固有复杂度，为在线MDP的实用算法设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for online episodic tabular Markov decision processes with unknown transition kernels, aiming for best-of-both-worlds guarantees together with data-dependent regret bounds. Recent work (Dann et al., 2023; Li et al., 2026) has shown that policy optimization can adapt to both adversarial and stochastic losses with first-order, second-order, and path-length bounds, but only under known transitions, leaving open whether such data-dependent guarantees are achievable by policy optimization when the transition kernel is unknown. We resolve this by developing a new algorithm based on optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader that attains these guarantees under unknown transitions. The key ingredient is a new design of optimistic $Q$-function estimators together with a data-dependent transition bonus that controls estimator bias through the loss-prediction error. Our analysis further identifies an unavoidable transition-dependent complexity term that captures the intrinsic cost of estimating the transition kernel. As a result, we obtain first-order, second-order, and path-length bounds with the transition-dependent complexity term while simultaneously achieving gap-dependent $\mathrm{polylog}(T)$ regret in the stochastic regime.

</details>


### [143] [Review Residuals: Update-Conditioned Residual Gating for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31859)
*Kyle Kramer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Review Residuals机制，通过学习输入相关的门控系数来调整残差连接中的更新权重，该门控同时依赖于当前状态和提议的更新。实验发现：该机制在深层网络中训练稳定（相较Highway风格的门控），且在较大规模模型（590M-1B参数）中显著优于标准残差连接和参数匹配的Highway门控，优势随模型规模增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准残差连接以固定系数1将每层的更新直接累加到主路径上，无法评估更新是否可靠。受人类因素原理中"独立验证"的启发，希望让网络在提交更新前先判断其可靠性。

Method: 提出Review Residuals，将残差连接修改为h_l = h_{l-1} + r_l * u_l，其中门控r_l = sigmoid(W[RMSNorm(h_{l-1}), RMSNorm(u_l)])。门控同时以当前状态和提议更新为条件，这是区别于此前门控残差的关键。对比了两种门控形式：凸组合（Highway风格）和加性恒等保持形式。

Result: 两个主要发现：(1) 深度稳定性：凸组合形式的门控会重新引入梯度消失问题，在约20层以上无法训练；而加性恒等保持形式在所有测试深度下稳定训练。(2) 随规模涌现：在60M-1B参数的五个规模上训练，小规模时无优势；在590M规模显著优于参数匹配的Highway门控和标准残差（p<0.05），1B时优势更大。优势随模型规模增长而非缩小。

Conclusion: Review Residuals在大规模模型训练中表现出明显优势，且其加性恒等保持的门控形式在深层网络中训练稳定，是一种有效的残差连接改进方案。

Abstract: Residual connections add every sublayer's proposed update with a fixed coefficient of one; the network never evaluates whether an update is reliable before committing it. Drawing on the human-factors principle of independent verification, we introduce Review Residuals, which scale each update by a learned, input-dependent gate conditioned on both the current state and the proposed update: h_l = h_{l-1} + r_l * u_l with r_l = sigmoid(W[RMSNorm(h_{l-1}), RMSNorm(u_l)]). Conditioning the gate on the update is the property that distinguishes it from prior gated and scaled residuals. We report two findings. First, a depth-stability result: a convex (Highway-style) form of the gate reintroduces vanishing gradients and fails to train beyond ~20 layers, whereas the additive, identity-preserving form trains stably at all depths we tested. Second, an emergence-with-scale result: trained from scratch across five sizes (60M-1B parameters, multi-seed), Review Residuals show no advantage at small scale but at 590M significantly outperform both a parameter-matched Highway gate and a parameter-matched standard residual (p<0.05), with a larger advantage at 1B. The benefit grows with model size rather than shrinking.

</details>


### [144] [Signed-Permutation Coordinate Transport for RMSNorm Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31963)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM残差流中的规范依赖性问题：LayerNorm具有置换规范S_d，而RMSNorm具有符号-置换规范B_d。为此提出符号边缘化匈牙利匹配方法，实现坐标保持传输。实验证明该方法在工具迁移（SAE重建、情感引导）、训练状态保持等方面显著优于传统置换对齐方法，并指出可解释性声明需明确规范才能复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM工作流中需要对坐标索引对象（如引导向量、SAE、神经元集等）进行检查点对齐，但现有方法忽略了一个关键问题：残差流的规范（gauge）依赖于架构——LayerNorm和RMSNorm的规范不同。不对齐规范会导致后续操作（如合并、迁移）失效或产生误导性结果。

Method: 1. 形式化定义了LayerNorm（置换规范S_d）和RMSNorm（符号-置换规范B_d = S_d ⋉ {±1}^d）的残差流规范。2. 提出符号边缘化匈牙利匹配（sign-marginalized Hungarian matching）来解决B_d规范下的对齐问题。3. 引入坐标保持传输（coordinate-preserving transport）作为核心操作，而非函数级合并。

Result: 坐标保持传输沿微调轨迹恢复91.1%的跨运行坐标（1500步），而端点匹配仅60.3%。工具迁移：TinyLlama SAE重建NMSE 0.004（B_d）vs 1.08（S_d）；Qwen情感引导保留95.8%效果 vs 17.2%；拒绝引导在S_d下会反转符号。训练状态保持：符号传输保留AdamW状态轨迹，置换传输则偏离。规范扫描审核显示可解释性声明仅在明确规范下可复现。

Conclusion: LLM残差流坐标对齐必须考虑架构依赖的规范结构。对于RMSNorm模型，仅做置换对齐是不够的，必须包括符号对齐。坐标保持传输是比函数级合并更基础的操作，对工具迁移、状态保持和可解释性均有深远影响。未来工作应关注规范的显式声明和自动对齐。

Abstract: Modern LLM workflows move coordinate-indexed objects across checkpoints: steering vectors, sparse autoencoders, top-$k$ neuron sets, attribution lists, and merge alignments. This is only well posed after fixing the model's residual-stream gauge, which we show is architecture-dependent: LayerNorm residual charts have permutation gauge $S_d$ (up to a global sign flip), while RMSNorm charts with generic per-channel gain have signed-permutation gauge $B_d = S_d \ltimes \{\pm 1\}^d$. Permutation-only alignment is therefore symmetry-incomplete for RMSNorm models. We introduce sign-marginalized Hungarian matching and prove a sharp failure mode: with decorrelated coordinates, raw signed-correlation matching has a structural permutation-accuracy ceiling at the positive-sign fraction of the true gauge, which sign-marginalization removes. We then make coordinate-preserving transport, not function-level merging, the primary object: composing saved-checkpoint local $B_d$ gauges along same-base fine-tuning trajectories recovers 91.1% of cross-run coordinates at 1500 steps versus 60.3% for endpoint matching, and the gain is not explained by merely routing through the base. The recovered gauge transfers tools that permutation-only alignment breaks: TinyLlama SAE reconstruction has NMSE 0.004 under $B_d$ versus 1.08 under $S_d$; Qwen sentiment steering preserves 95.8% of its effect versus 17.2%; refusal steering reverses sign under $S_d$; coordinate-preserving merges behave the same way. The same covariance governs stateful training: signed transport of AdamW state preserves the resumed trajectory, while permutation-only state follows a different one from a functionally identical checkpoint. Finally, gauge-sweep audits show index-level interpretability claims are reproducible only relative to an explicit gauge.

</details>


### [145] [Relational and Sequential Conformal Inference for Energy Time Series over Graphs via Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31804)
*Keivan Faghih Niresi,Alice Cicirello,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STOIC框架，将图神经网络点预测与表格基础模型的上下文学习相结合，用于能源系统的时空共形预测，无需重新训练即可生成具有统计覆盖保证的可靠不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能源需求预测中，点预测不足以支持风险决策，而传统共形预测方法未能充分利用能源系统的复杂时空结构。需要一种能同时捕捉时序依赖和关系依赖且具有统计覆盖保证的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 首先使用STGNN生成点预测，然后将时空残差重构为表格表示，再利用表格基础模型通过上下文学习（零样本校准）对预测区间进行校准，无需针对特定任务重新训练。

Result: 在五个多样化基准数据集（包括合成模拟、真实电力网络和区域供热网络）上，STOIC在所有数据集上一致优于现有共形预测基线，提供更可靠、更鲁棒的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: STOIC有效融合了图神经网络和表格基础模型的优势，为图结构能源时序数据提供了一种零样本、无需重训练的鲁棒不确定性量化方案，提升了风险感知决策和电网运行可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate energy demand forecasting is essential for the reliable operation and planning of modern sustainable energy systems. Spatial-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) have recently achieved strong performance in point forecasting by jointly modeling temporal dynamics and relational dependencies across interconnected energy nodes. However, in real-world energy systems, accurate point forecasts alone are insufficient, as operators also require reliable uncertainty estimates to support risk-aware decision-making, grid stability, and operational planning under uncertainty. Conformal prediction provides a principled and model-agnostic framework for uncertainty quantification with statistical coverage guarantees, making it particularly attractive for safety-critical energy applications. However, existing conformal prediction approaches often fail to fully capture the complex spatial-temporal structure of energy systems. To address these limitations, we propose STOIC (Spatial-Temporal Graph Conformal Prediction with In-Context Learning), a novel framework that integrates graph-based forecasting with the zero-shot calibration capabilities of tabular foundation models. STOIC first generates point forecasts using an STGNN and subsequently reformulates spatial-temporal residuals into a tabular representation suitable for in-context learning. Leveraging a tabular foundation model, STOIC calibrates prediction intervals without task-specific retraining, effectively capturing both sequential and relational dependencies. We evaluate STOIC on five diverse benchmarks, including synthetic simulations as well as real-world electricity and district heating networks. Across all datasets, STOIC consistently outperforms existing conformal prediction baselines, delivering more reliable and robust uncertainty estimates for complex graph-structured energy time series.

</details>


### [146] [SemRF: A Semantic Reference Frame for Residual-Stream Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32022)
*Jian Gu,Aldeida Aleti,Chunyang Chen,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义参考框架（SemRF），通过锚点固定消除嵌入与解嵌入之间的测量漂移，将残差流转化为深度语义轨迹，并利用Voronoi图、离散样条路径和动作最小化原理分析语言模型的层间计算，建立轨迹复杂度与参数效率之间的条件联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差流分析需要在各层间使用可比较的读取坐标，但嵌入锚点与解嵌入读取在所选跨度上可能存在不一致，导致表观运动反映的是测量漂移而非实际计算。因此需要一种形式化方法将语义测量与残差动力学分离开来。

Method: 提出SemRF形式化方法：固定语义锚点，通过伪逆绑定实现精确同步；基于限制性双可逆性建立稳定的语义基坐标、失真界和近恒等变化。利用锚点诱导语义Voronoi图，定义层间步长、贡献剖面和不平衡诊断指标；通过Voronoi迹定义边缘松弛管，在管内寻找最小作用量路径，将其表述为离散样条方程。

Result: 当管内非空且二次权值为正时，最小作用量路径唯一且服从离散样条方程；低曲率意味着分段线性可压缩性和局部知识密度，即低迹复杂度对应更少的语义节点；在参数到轨迹的映射下，低作用量低复杂度的迹使用更少的语义自由度。

Conclusion: SemRF为语言模型残差流分析提供了严格的语义坐标系框架，通过轨迹复杂度和作用量建立了与参数效率的条件性联系，但保证成立的条件是受控的接口误差和显式管约束下的小投影残差。

Abstract: Residual-stream analysis asks how language-model computation evolves across depth, but intermediate decoding requires comparable readout coordinates across layers. If embedding anchors and unembedding readout disagree on the chosen span, apparent motion may reflect measurement drift rather than computation. We introduce \emph{Semantic Reference Frames} (SemRF), an anchor-based formalism separating semantic measurement from residual dynamics. A SemRF fixes anchors and measures states against them. Pseudo-inverse tying gives exact synchronization; under restricted bi-invertibility, SemRF yields stable semantic-basis coordinates, distortion bounds, and near-identity changes. With the frame fixed, residual computation becomes a depthwise semantic trajectory. The anchors induce a semantic Voronoi diagram: distance, or evidence such as logits, assigns each layer to a coarse cell, while coordinates retain within-cell motion and margins. We define layerwise steps, contribution profiles, and imbalance diagnostics, then use the Voronoi trace to define a margin-relaxed tube. The canonical trace is the minimum-action path inside this tube; when nonempty with positive quadratic weight, it is unique and obeys a discrete spline equation away from active constraints. Excess action controls step, curvature, and profile mismatch. Low curvature implies piecewise-linear compressibility and local knowledge density: lower trace complexity means fewer semantic knots. Through the parameter-to-trajectory map, this gives a conditional link to parameter efficiency: among admissible settings fitting data, lower-action and lower-complexity traces use fewer semantic degrees of freedom. The guarantees require controlled interface error and small projection residual under explicit tube constraints.

</details>


### [147] [Geometry-Preserving Orthonormal Initialization for Low-Rank Adaptation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31813)
*Ruijia Zhang,Jiacheng Zhu,Hanqing Zhu,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对低秩适配（LoRA）及其变体在基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中的初始化问题展开理论分析，发现正交初始化在RLVR中能最小化LoRA与全微调之间的差距，并据此提出几何保持正交初始化方法（RLPO和RLMO），在数学推理基准上稳定训练并超越标准LoRA，同时解释了PiSSA和MiLoRA在RLVR中表现不佳的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA及其变体在监督微调（SFT）范式下表现优异，但在基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中的效果和行为尚不明确。特别是PiSSA和MiLoRA在SFT下优于标准LoRA，但在RLVR下却可能表现更差甚至训练不稳定，表明RLVR中低秩矩阵的初始化方式是一个关键但尚未解决的问题。

Method: 首先从理论上分析LoRA在RLVR中的行为，证明正交初始化能最小化LoRA输出与全微调之间的差距。基于这一理论洞察，提出几何保持正交初始化方法，衍生出两个新变体RLPO和RLMO。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，所提出的正交初始化方法能够稳定RLVR训练，且性能优于标准LoRA，与PiSSA和MiLoRA形成对比。同时，统一的理论分析也解释了PiSSA和MiLoRA在RLVR中表现不佳的原因。

Conclusion: 正交初始化是RLVR场景下LoRA适配的关键因素，几何保持正交初始化方法有效提升了训练稳定性和性能，为RLVR中的参数高效微调提供了理论指导和实用方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and its variants enable parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models under the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) paradigm. However, their efficacy and behavior under Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) are less well understood. In particular, two structurally initialized LoRA variants, PiSSA and MiLoRA, which outperform standard LoRA under SFT, can underperform standard LoRA under RLVR and may even exhibit training instability. These observations suggest that how to initialize the low-rank matrices in RLVR remains unclear. In this work, we develop a theoretical analysis of LoRA in RLVR, showing that orthonormal initialization achieves the minimal gap between LoRA outcome and that of full fine-tuning. Guided by this insight, we propose geometry-preserving orthonormal initialization for low-rank adaptation in RLVR, leading to two new variants, RLPO and RLMO. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed orthonormal initialization stabilizes RLVR training and outperforms standard LoRA, contrasting with PiSSA and MiLoRA. Finally, our unified analysis for LoRA initialization also explains why PiSSA and MiLoRA can underperform in RLVR, which may be of independent interest. Code and checkpoints are publicly available at https://github.com/Richard-ZZZ/geometry-preserving-orthonormal-init-rlvr.

</details>


### [148] [QVal: Cheaply Evaluating Dense Supervision Signals for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32034)
*Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez,Matteo Merler,Ilze Amanda Auzina,Joschka Strüber,Ameya Prabhu,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QVal，一个无需训练的测试平台，用于直接评估密集监督信号的质量。通过对21种密集监督方法、4个环境和6种模型后端的基准测试发现：简单的提示基线方法优于近期提出的复杂密集监督方法，且性能按方法家族聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集监督方法评估依赖下游训练性能，这既昂贵又混杂了训练工程干扰因素，且不同方法家族因训练设置不同而无法公平比较，缺乏统一的基准测试平台。

Method: 提出QVal评估框架，通过计算方法给出的分数与强参考策略的Q值之间的一致性（Q-alignment）来直接评估密集监督信号质量，无需训练。具体衡量方法对状态-动作对排序是否与Q值排序一致。

Result: 对21种密集监督方法、4个环境、6种开源模型后端进行1200+次实验，发现简单提示基线方法显著优于近期复杂方法，且性能按方法家族聚类。该结论跨模型大小、环境和观测模态保持一致。

Conclusion: QVal实现了训练前直接评估密集监督信号，分离了信号质量与工程混杂因素，为方法开发提供了低成本迭代工具。简单提示基线优于复杂方法这一发现对当前密集监督研究方向具有重要启示。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act over long horizons, where a single trajectory can contain hundreds or thousands of actions. In these settings, outcome-only rewards provide too sparse guidance, failing to inform the model about the goodness of intermediate actions. Dense supervision methods aim to solve this problem by scoring intermediate steps, from intrinsic confidence to self-distillation and embedding similarities. However, it is common practice to evaluate them by measuring the downstream performance of a training pipeline that integrates them.
